*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个部署在开放世界学习（OWL）异常检测管道下游的大语言模型（LLM）智能体层，旨在为上游检测结果提供自然语言解释、置信度排序的批判意见以及新发现异常簇的可读命名，从而弥合OWL管道在工业部署中的可解释性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWL管道虽能回答"发生了什么"（what），但无法解释"模型为何如此判断"（why）或"操作员下一步该做什么"（what to do next），也无法为新发现的异常簇赋予人类可读的名称。这种可解释性缺口阻碍了OWL管道在真实工业环境中的部署。

Method: 在已有OWL管道下游增设LLM智能体层（使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B混合专家模型，通过NVIDIA NIM服务），接收结构化传感器指标及上游分类/新颖性断言，输出自然语言理由、置信度排序的批判意见以及检测到的新颖事件的统一命名。在3W数据集的989个真实井文件片段上进行了三项研究评估。

Result: 在全部9类分类任务上达到35.1% top-1 / 63.9% top-3准确率（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]）；在7个探测类上达到71.7% top-2验证准确率 [64.8, 77.6]，精确率0.91 [0.84, 0.95]；新颖性检测达89.7% [87.0, 91.9]，并在7个隐藏类中的5个上实现了稳定的簇命名。

Conclusion: 该LLM智能体并非独立分类器，其核心角色是：确认传感器证据支持的上游决策、以可审计的传感器语言给出判断理由、在上游标签不可信时标记分歧、为新发现异常簇赋予统一的人类可读标签，从而有效弥合可解释性缺口。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [234] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus 是一个仅使用电阻式压力阵列数据进行文本查询的开源触觉表征模型，在 STAG 基准上以 187 条训练记录达到 0.771 top-1 准确率，匹敌甚至超越监督式 CNN。核心秘诀是小数据策略：MAE 预训练 + 传感器自校准仿射变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前触觉表征学习主要集中于光学传感器（成像变形凝胶），而最廉价、部署最广泛的电阻式压力阵列却被忽视。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索仅从压力数据回答文本查询的可行性，并验证小数据条件下的有效训练策略。

Method: 提出 Tactus 模型：在 144k 未标注同传感器帧上进行掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练，仅使用 187 条训练记录进行下游任务；关键发现是引入传感器自身的校准仿射变换，其带来的精度提升超过所有架构改进之和。模型无需训练分类头，直接通过文本查询进行零样本识别。还尝试了跨传感器预训练池化、视觉联合训练等消融实验。

Result: 在 STAG 基准（27 物体、留出记录）上，四次运行平均 top-1 为 0.771±0.062，top-3 达 0.935，匹敌或超越数据集的有监督闭集 CNN（0.76）。错误集中在少数接触模糊类别，与文本-目标几何特征无显著相关（Spearman ρ ≤ 0.05）。两帧即可恢复八帧精度的 89%。负面结果：跨传感器预训练池化无增益，视觉联合训练损害触觉性能，输入管线归一化错误曾静默丢弃 97% 动态范围。

Conclusion: Tactus 证明仅用廉价的电阻式压力阵列即可实现与光学传感器相媲美的语言引导触觉识别，小数据条件下 MAE 预训练加传感器校准仿射是关键要素。模型、权重及代码已开源，为触觉表征学习提供了实用的基线。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [235] [Sample Complexity of Multicalibration for Multilevel Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04288)
*Jiuyao Lu,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Aleksandr Podkopaev,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多校准（multicalibration）在多个有序可识别属性序列上的推广，建立了样本复杂度的紧界：在正则条件下，达到多校准误差ε需要̃Ω(ε^{-(k+2)})个样本，并给出了一个随机化学习器，其样本复杂度为O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)，从而对多项式大小的群族实现̃Θ(ε^{-(k+2)})的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校准要求预测器在给定自身预测的条件下无偏，多校准将此要求扩展到多个群组。然而许多预测任务涉及同一条件结果分布的多个关联特征（如方差依赖于均值、偏度依赖于均值和方差、条件风险价值依赖于分位数）。现有框架（如Bayes对）未能充分覆盖这类有序可识别属性的序列校准问题，因此需要建立统一的理论框架并刻画其样本复杂度。

Method: 作者将k个属性的序列校准问题形式化为：每个属性在前序属性固定的条件下可识别。在此框架下，对任意固定的k≥2，在正则条件下建立了样本复杂度的匹配上下界：下界通过构造性证明给出̃Ω(ε^{-(k+2)})；上界则通过设计一个随机化学习器实现O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)的样本量。该框架涵盖Bayes对但不要求属性源自单一损失函数，并在三个典型实例上进行实例化验证。

Result: （1）下界：即使仅有多项式对数量级的二值群组，达到多校准误差ε也需要̃Ω(ε^{-(k+2)})个样本。（2）上界：对任意有限群族G，随机化学习器使用O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)个样本即可实现多校准。（3）综合上下界：对多项式大小的群族，样本复杂度为̃Θ(ε^{-(k+2)})。（4）在三个典型例子（均值-方差、均值-方差-偏度、分位数-CVaR）中验证了理论的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文为有序可识别属性序列的多校准问题建立了首个紧的样本复杂度刻画。结果表明，随着属性数量k增加，样本需求呈多项式增长（̃Θ(ε^{-(k+2)})），且该界对多项式大小的群族是紧的。该框架为理解条件分布多属性联合校准的理论极限提供了基础工具。

Abstract: Calibration requires a predictor to be unbiased after conditioning on its own predictions. Multicalibration asks for this guarantee simultaneously across a collection of groups. Many prediction tasks ask for several related features of the same conditional outcome distribution: variance is defined relative to the mean, skewness relative to both mean and variance, and conditional value at risk relative to a quantile. We study multicalibration for a sequence of $k$ properties in which each property is identifiable once the preceding properties are fixed. This framework includes Bayes pairs but does not require the properties to arise from a single loss.
  For every fixed $k\ge2$, we establish matching upper and lower sample-complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors under regularity conditions. Even with only polylogarithmically many binary groups, achieving multicalibration error $\varepsilon$ requires $\widetildeΩ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ samples. Conversely, for any finite group family $\mathcal G$, we give a randomized learner using $O(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\log|\mathcal G|)$ samples. Thus the sample complexity is $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ for polynomial-size group families. We instantiate the theory for three canonical examples.

</details>


### [236] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在航空发动机剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测中的应用，系统评估了良性异质性（不同运行条件）和对抗性异质性（投毒攻击）两类挑战。研究发现共享表征个性化方法可弥合约70%的本地-集中式RMSE差距，而基于传感器值注入的后门攻击对标准聚合方法成功率高达94.9%。Krum是唯一能抵御协调攻击的聚合器，将个性化与鲁棒聚合相结合可在轻微精度代价下恢复鲁棒性（攻击成功率降至2.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空机队运营商希望利用联邦学习联合训练RUL预测模型以保护数据隐私，但面临两大挑战：（1）不同运营商的运行条件和故障模式不同（良性异质性）；（2）恶意参与者可能提交投毒更新（对抗性异质性）。现有研究通常将两者分开处理，缺乏在安全关键场景下的统一评估框架，且准确率指标无法单独证明模型安全性。

Method: 使用多任务一维卷积神经网络和C-MAPSS基准数据集的结构化非IID划分进行受控安全评估。对比四种良性异质性解决方案（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务器端重加权、本地微调）；评估五种攻击（包括一种物理驱动的传感器值后门攻击，旨在掩盖发动机退化）对四种聚合方法（标准平均FedAvg、Krum、中位数聚合、Trimmed Mean）的效果；进一步分析个性化与鲁棒聚合的组合策略。

Result: 共享表征个性化弥合了约70%的本地-集中式RMSE差距，优于近端正则化（21%）和服务器端重加权（10%）。传感器后门攻击对标准平均聚合的攻击成功率达94.9%，而干净数据准确率无统计显著变化，证明准确率不能单独作为安全认证指标。Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一能抵御协调攻击的聚合器。个性化本身不提供保护，但将个性化与鲁棒聚合结合可使攻击成功率降至2.8%，仅付出轻微精度代价。结果在不同客户端数量和更困难的六工况数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 在安全关键的航空RUL预测中，必须显式评估攻击成功率而非仅依赖准确率指标。共享表征个性化是应对良性异质性的最有效方法，而Krum是对抗性场景下最鲁棒的聚合器。个性化与鲁棒聚合的结合揭示了两者之间的折衷关系——鲁棒更新选择与协作表征学习存在张力。研究代码和数据划分已开源以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [237] [ArborEnum: Decision Tree Rashomon Sets over Continuous Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04310)
*Zakk Heile,Hayden McTavish,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个能在连续特征下精确枚举决策树拉什蒙集（Rashomon set）的算法，无需事先对数据进行二值化处理，并开发了近似枚举和随时算法，实现数量级加速同时保持近乎完美的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉什蒙效应揭示了多个模型可在同一任务上达到近乎相同的性能，这对鲁棒性、特征重要性和可定制性具有重要意义。然而，现有决策树拉什蒙集的精确枚举方法必须依赖数据二值化预处理，这要么限制了树的分裂能力，要么极大增加了组合问题的复杂度，无法有效利用连续特征的有序结构。

Method: 作者提出了三种算法：(1) 首个精确枚举算法，直接利用连续特征的排序结构枚举决策树拉什蒙集；(2) 一种松弛方法用于近似枚举；(3) 一种随时算法（anytime algorithm），逐步细化候选阈值集合，产生越来越精细的近似结果，最终收敛到连续特征拉什蒙集。

Result: 实验表明，粗粒度二值化会遗漏大量决策树、重要特征和预测多重性（predictive multiplicity）；所提算法相比现有枚举方法实现了数个数量级的加速，近似方法在保持近乎完美召回率的同时进一步提升了速度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了连续特征下决策树拉什蒙集的精确枚举，消除了对数据二值化的依赖，通过精确、近似和随时算法三种方案，在计算效率与枚举完整性之间提供了灵活的选择，显著推动了拉什蒙集在实际应用中的可用性。

Abstract: The Rashomon effect describes the phenomenon that many models can achieve nearly equivalent performance on the same learning task, with significant ramifications for robustness, feature importance, and customizability. These use cases motivate the computation of Rashomon sets: the set of all models whose regularized loss is near-optimal. Decision trees are one of the few model classes for which Rashomon sets can be fully enumerated, but this computation has always been conditional on a binarization of the original data, either restricting which splits each tree is allowed to make or substantially increasing the complexity of an already difficult combinatorial problem. We introduce the first algorithm that exactly enumerates decision-tree Rashomon sets while exploiting the ordered structure of continuous features. We further develop a relaxation for approximate enumeration and an anytime algorithm that progressively refines the set of candidate thresholds, producing increasingly detailed approximations that converge to the continuous-feature Rashomon set. Experiments show that coarse binarization can miss many trees, important features, and predictive multiplicity; our algorithms achieve orders-of-magnitude speedups over existing enumeration methods, with approximations providing further speedups while maintaining near-perfect recall.

</details>


### [238] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RRQ 是一种训练后量化框架，通过将权重表示为一个低比特量化基础值加上一系列量化残差修正，在单一检查点中支持 2/4/6/8 比特多种有效精度，构建速度快且无需校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法通常需要为每个目标比特宽度分别存储一个检查点，无法灵活适应多样化的部署约束。作者希望实现从单一检查点支持多精度推理，从而在精度、内存和吞吐量之间灵活权衡。

Method: 提出 Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ)：先从 PTQ 或 RTN 获得 2-bit 基础模型，然后通过 RTN 逐步叠加轻量级 2-bit 残差，依次构造 4-bit、6-bit、8-bit 表示。整个过程无需校准数据，也不涉及联合多比特优化。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上，完整 2/4/6/8-bit 全 RTN 包构建仅需 1,293 秒，比 MatGPTQ 快 3.3 倍。在六个最新 LLM 上，6-bit 和 8-bit 精度具有竞争力，4-bit 表现与模型相关。

Conclusion: RRQ 以极低的构建成本实现了单检查点多精度支持，为 LLM 的高效灵活部署提供了一种实用的 PTQ 方案。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [239] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMP 提出了一种周期感知的多尺度补丁混合架构，通过自适应周期学习、时间引导的补丁混合以及多分辨率残差建模，解决了现有预报模型在周期选择僵化、补丁处理均匀、单一尺度建模等方面的不足，在多个长时预测基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有周期感知预测模型存在三个主要局限：(1) 依赖数据集级别的单一预设周期，无法应对周期随时间变化或多种周期共存的情形；(2) 基于补丁（patch）的模型对所有补丁位置统一处理，忽略了远离预测边界的补丁需要更广泛上下文、而近邻补丁应更直接保留信息的差异；(3) 去除周期成分后的残差动态可能跨越多个时间分辨率，单一尺度不足以充分描述。

Method: 提出 CAMP（Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer），包含三个核心模块：(1) **自适应周期学习模块**：针对每个输入窗口独立识别主导频率，无需预定义周期长度，同时生成历史和未来的周期成分；(2) **时间引导补丁混合器**：引入位置依赖的精炼机制，使较早的补丁融合更广泛的时序上下文，同时保护靠近预测边界的近邻信息；(3) **多分辨率残差建模**：对去周期后的残差通过时间对齐的多分辨率表示进行建模，在同一框架内捕捉不同时间尺度的互补动态。

Result: 在7个长时预测基准上，CAMP 在6个数据集上取得了最优平均 MSE，在6个数据集上取得最优或并列最优的 MAE；在4个 PEMS 交通基准的16种设置中，CAMP 获得了最高的 MSE 胜出次数。

Conclusion: CAMP 通过周期自适应、位置感知补丁混合与多尺度残差建模三项创新，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，在长时序列预测任务上实现了全面且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [240] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LaPrune 提出一种精确预算可微的 top-k 选择层，通过 LapSum 障碍保持选择质量，利用归一化二阶矩约束使掩码从等质量分配逐步逼近硬 top-k，具有尺度不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 top-k 选择方法中，硬选择阻断梯度，而连续松弛方法往往将掩码硬度与被选择质量耦合在一起，无法在保持可微性的同时精确控制选择预算和硬度。

Method: 提出 LaPrune 层：(1) LapSum 障碍函数精确保持每行的选择质量总和；(2) 归一化二阶矩约束驱动掩码从稠密等质量分配向硬 top-k 收敛；(3) 推导了饱和比例的人口预测、近二值极限律以及近零比例的紧最坏情况保证；(4) 归一化硬度参数对分数尺度不变，而固定 LapSum 温度不具备此性质。

Result: 得到饱和比例的人口预测、近二值极限律以及近零比例的紧最坏情况保证；证明归一化硬度参数具有分数尺度不变性，优于固定温度的 LapSum。

Conclusion: LaPrune 实现了精确预算、可微且尺度不变的 top-k 选择，为稀疏模型的软硬选择提供了理论保证更优的连续松弛方案。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [241] [Non-asymptotic implicit bias of logistic regression at early-stage gradient descent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04382)
*Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了梯度下降在指数尾损失函数下的早期隐式偏差现象，证明了参数向量在远快于渐近收敛速率的迭代次数内（$O(\exp(\exp(-\delta)))$）即可与最大间隔方向实现弱对齐，并证明该界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论表明，线性分类器在梯度下降下的最大间隔隐式偏差是渐近现象，且收敛速度极慢。然而实践中常观察到参数向量在远少于渐近速率所需的迭代次数内就与最大间隔方向呈正相关。本文旨在从理论上揭示这一"早期对齐"现象的机制。

Method: 作者通过直接追踪梯度下降动力学中的径向流和切向流，在不对梯度下降轨迹做渐近展开的前提下，利用数据集几何结构分析对齐动力学，建立了早期弱对齐的理论保证。

Result: 证明了在 $O(\exp(\exp(-\delta)))$ 次迭代内，参数向量可与最大间隔方向实现 $\delta$-弱对齐（$\delta>0$ 为容许的对齐误差），且该界是紧的。这显著快于经典渐近收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该工作为"训练更久，泛化更好"的经验现象提供了新的理论视角，揭示了早期弱对齐的动力学机制，关键洞察在于绕过渐近展开、直接分析对齐动力学。

Abstract: Gradient descent has been of particular interest in modern machine learning beyond sole focus on optimization. Implicit bias emerging from optimization, though not being encoded by the learning objective, often prevents from overfitting to spurious patterns. A typical instance is the max-margin implicit bias of a linear classifier, widely established for exponentially tailed loss functions. Even after having a given dataset separated, the parameter vector continues to evolve towards the max-margin direction asymptotically along the gradient descent dynamics. This phenomenon corroborates a frequent empirical observation of "train longer, generalize better." However, the max-margin convergence is an asymptotic phenomenon, and what is worse, this asymptotic convergence rate is significantly slower than pure convex optimization. Even so, the parameter vector along gradient descent dynamics commonly correlates with the max-margin direction positively (though not exactly) within considerably fewer iterations than the asymptotic rate. By shedding another light on this classical problem, this work aims to understand the mechanism of this early-stage alignment phenomenon. Our theoretical results demonstrate that the parameter vector weakly aligns with the max-margin direction within $O(\exp(\exp(-δ)))$ iterations, where $δ>0$ is the permissible alignment error, which is shown to be tight. By tracking the radial and tangential flows, our proof operates on the alignment dynamics directly with dataset geometry and gets rid of the asymptotic expansion, which is a key insight to establishing faster weak alignment.

</details>


### [242] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SJEPA框架，通过学习预测性表征并约束其诱导动力学，使隐含的转移模型既保持预测精度，又能被紧凑的符号-神经混合规则所描述。核心原则是“学习最简的充分动力学”，通过表征约束防止坍缩、算子压缩偏好低复杂度符号规则，并引入正则化神经修正处理符号语法外的残差动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的转移模型通常是不透明的神经网络黑箱，缺乏可解释性。作者希望构建一种无需重构的框架，学习到的预测表征能够诱导出可被紧凑符号描述的动力系统，从而在保持预测能力的同时获得可解释的动力学规律。

Method: 提出SJEPA（Symbolic Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture）框架。其混合转移模型由一个符号定律和一个正则化神经网络修正项组成。核心机制包括：(1) 表征约束以保留信息量、非坍缩的预测坐标；(2) 算子压缩偏好低复杂度的符号-神经转移；(3) 诱导动力学复杂度作为形式化准则。框架支持交替表征-方程联合学习，也支持在固定表征上拟合符号动力学。同时分析了预测坐标的非可识别性问题，并证明无约束算子压缩会导致表征坍缩的捷径。

Result: 在受控摆实验上，联合学习发现的符号动力学相比事后拟合（post-hoc fitting）具有显著更低的长期 rollout 误差和发散率，且符号规则更简洁。无约束一步诊断实验验证了预测中的坍缩捷径。在语法误设情况下，修正正则化成功保留了可表示的符号机制，并将神经网络引导至残差动力学。实验揭示了预测精度、表征质量、符号简约性和符号-神经分配之间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: SJEPA成功实现了预测表征学习与符号动力学发现的统一，验证了“最简充分动力学”原则的有效性。该方法在可解释性、预测精度和模型复杂度之间提供了可调节的权衡机制。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [243] [Discretization and Statistical Consistency of Functional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04531)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在函数流匹配框架下，证明了有限条件速度目标的强L²收敛性，处理了非嵌套条件σ-代数的传感器极限问题。给出了正交投影的定量界、点传感器扩展、端到端Wasserstein界，并通过数值算例和理论分析建立了显式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数流匹配在实际中只能通过有限个系数或点值实现，但在散点或自适应细化下，条件σ-代数不满足嵌套性，导致经典鞅收敛理论无法为传感器极限提供理论保证。需要建立新的收敛理论来桥接有限维实现与无穷维函数空间的差距。

Method: 作者首先针对任意强一致有限秩重构序列，证明有限条件速度目标在L²意义下的强收敛性；对正交投影给出定量界，并通过正则性空间扩展到点传感器情形。在学习流方面，将有限维流直接耦合到总体叠加路径，导出端到端Wasserstein界，且无需总体有限维ODE的唯一性假设。随后为归一化求积神经算子验证了传感器无关常数，通过显式幅度递推处理全局Lipschitz激活函数。最后利用Bernstein论证给出固定模型维度下O(n⁻¹)量级的超额风险项，并在可精确实现的截断高斯缩放特例中给出显式端到端速率。

Result: 理论结果包括：有限条件速度的L²强收敛性；正交投影和点传感器扩展的定量界；不依赖唯一性的端到端Wasserstein界；非交换迹类高斯例子中，投影限制下边界乘子为0，精确条件下为0.72；算子实现项通过空间正则性-容积证书闭合；Bernstein论证导出超额风险项为O(n⁻¹)；截断高斯缩放特例给出显式端到端收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文为函数流匹配在有限维实现下的收敛性提供了严格理论框架，填补了非嵌套条件σ-代数情形下传感器极限的理论空白。通过定量界和显式速率，为函数流匹配在实际应用中的可靠性和收敛速度提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Functional flow matching is posed on distributions of functions but implemented from finitely many coefficients or point values. Under scattered or adaptive refinement, the resulting conditioning sigma-algebras need not be nested, so martingale convergence does not justify the sensor limit. We prove strong $L^2$ convergence of finite conditional velocity targets for every strongly consistent sequence of finite-rank reconstructions, with quantitative bounds for orthogonal projections and a point-sensor extension through a regularity space. For learned flows, coupling directly to a population superposition path yields an end-to-end Wasserstein bound without assuming uniqueness of the population finite-dimensional ODE. We verify sensor-independent constants for a normalized quadrature neural operator, including globally Lipschitz activations through an explicit magnitude recurrence. A noncommuting trace-class Gaussian example gives boundary multiplier $0$ under projected restriction and $0.72$ under exact conditioning. A spatial regularity--cubature certificate closes the operator-realization term, a Bernstein argument gives a $\widetilde{O}(n^{-1})$ excess-risk term for fixed model dimension and envelopes, and an exactly realizable clipped Gaussian scaling specialization yields an explicit end-to-end rate.

</details>


### [244] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOVA-KV 将KV缓存量化建模为变换编码问题，基于标定统计量推导出注意力感知的最优变换（密钥变换非正交且满足广义Parseval关系），结合MSE最优向量量化器与等体积分区策略，在每元素2比特下显著恢复长上下文检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码中，KV缓存的加载带宽构成吞吐量瓶颈。现有2比特量化方法要么数据无关，要么未从失真准则导出变换，且依赖Hadamard旋转（方差均衡而非能量压缩）和固定宽度标量量化器（低码率下次优），亟需一种基于率失真理论的最优变换编码方案。

Method: 将KV缓存量化形式化为变换编码问题，失真定义为注意力乘积的误差。基于高分辨率模型从标定统计量推导键和值的闭式最优变换；证明最优键变换非正交但满足广义Parseval关系，使注意力感知失真等价于变换域MSE；采用MSE最优向量量化器；通过等体积分区使等大小码本达到变码率最优，满足固定每令牌比特数约束。

Result: 在每元素2比特条件下，NOVA-KV在可比吞吐量下恢复了标量量化方法在长上下文检索任务中损失的大部分精度。

Conclusion: NOVA-KV通过注意力感知的变换编码框架，在严格比特约束下实现了KV缓存压缩的率失真最优，显著优于现有标量量化方法。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [245] [An entropic explanation of insistence on sameness in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04616)
*Przemysław Śliwiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论的框架，将自闭症中的"固守同一性"解释为个体减少惊奇与不确定性的行为模式，并通过条件熵度量 $D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)$ 进行形式化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自闭症中的固守同一性现象提供一种统一的信息论解释，将其纳入"个体试图减少惊奇与不确定性"这一更一般行为模式的框架中，并尝试为自闭症给出新的定义和量化手段。

Method: 基于信息论，定义度量 $D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)$，其中 $R$ 为随机刺激序列，$M$ 为记忆系统，$H(R|M)$ 解释为惊奇，$H(M|R)$ 解释为不确定性。推理得出最小化该度量的两种途径：要么学习 $R$（将知识存入 $M$），要么将 $R$ 限制在已知的 $M$ 范围内；后者即为固守同一性。进一步将该框架用于量化多种概念、导出照护指南，并提出基于类图灵测试的验证方案。

Result: 该框架能够量化惊奇、不确定性、感官过载与剥夺、焦虑、舒适区、失望、定向障碍、刻板、僵化、过度精确等概念；可导出一套用于学习疗法和日常照护的指南，并将其定义为优化算法，甚至可编程部署于机器人照护者；提出了一种无需自闭症个体参与实验的图灵测试式验证方法。

Conclusion: 若该框架得到正面验证，将为旨在提升自闭症个体基本日常生活自理能力的疗法提供形式化基础和设计指南。

Abstract: An information theory-based framework is proposed in attempt to explain insistence on sameness in autism as an instance of a general behavior pattern in which an individual tries to reduce surprise and uncertainty. It offers a new definition of autism as an impairment in which cognitive functions are restricted to discrimination, memorization and prediction of tangible properties of the environment. An analogy between insistence on sameness and constrained minimization of the entropy metric is observed and examined for a set of assumptions that describe cognitive limitations of a person with autism. The metric is given by the formula $D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)$, where $R$ represents sequences of random stimuli, $M$ is a memory that stores and retrieves them, and where $H(.|.)$ denotes their conditional entropies interpreted as surprise and uncertainty, respectively. It is first inferred that to minimize the metric an individual can learn about $R$ (and store that knowledge in $M$) or can restrict $R$ to the already known $M$. Then, it is concluded that insistence on sameness is a manifestation of the latter. Moreover, it is shown that the proposed framework: (1) Helps to quantify the concepts of surprise, uncertainty, sensory overload and deprivation, anxiety, comfort zone, disappointment, disorientation, pedantry, rigidness, observance or aberrant precision. (2) Leads to a list of guidelines for learning therapies and daily care routines, and allows them to be defined as optimization algorithms and implemented as programs for robotic live-in caregivers. (3) Can be validated with the help of a Turing test-like approach that requires no experiments involving individuals with autism. The framework-if positively validated-will provide formal foundations and design guidelines for therapies aimed at improving self-reliance of individuals with autism in basic activities of daily living.

</details>


### [246] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时空图Transformer框架，结合图神经网络捕获空间相关性、利用Transformer自注意力机制建模长程时间依赖，用于边缘计算中的流量预测。在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多个预测时段上一致优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM、GCN-GRU等基线模型，能有效支持主动式资源供给并降低过载风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中流量呈现强空间相关性和长程时间依赖性，现有循环预测方法在非平稳条件下难以建模长时域流量演变，需要一种能联合建模时空依赖关系的有效方法。

Method: 提出时空图Transformer框架：采用图神经网络（GNN）捕获服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer自注意力机制从历史流量观测中学习长程时间模式，并将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦，实现大规模时空流量建模。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上，所提图Transformer在多个预测时段上一致超越GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM、GCN-GRU等基于循环网络的图基线模型；生成的预测能够实现更有效的主动式资源供给，相比被动管理策略显著降低过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强注意力机制在构建智能自适应边缘计算系统中具有显著潜力，时空图Transformer为边缘流量预测提供了有效的解决方案，有助于推动主动式资源管理的发展。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [247] [Personalized Federated Sparse Adaptation of Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04695)
*Priyanka Nihalchandani,Naman Srivastava,Varun Ojha,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对建筑能耗预测中数据隐私性强、分布式且高度非IID的挑战，本文提出一种个性化联邦稀疏适配框架，在预训练时序基础模型后接入异质时序混合专家适配器，通过序列级路由器为每个168小时上下文窗口选择top-k专家。在50栋建筑和三种TSFM骨干上的实验表明，个性化策略一致优于全局联邦学习和本地训练，同时揭示了路由行为中客户端级别和骨干级别的专家专长差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗预测场景中，电表数据具有私密性、分布式和高度非IID的特性，使得联邦适配时序基础模型具有吸引力。然而单一参数共享策略无法同时满足所有预训练TSFM和建筑客户端的需求：完全共享适配器会抑制建筑特定的时序行为，完全本地适配则丢弃了跨建筑迁移能力。因此需要一种既能保留跨建筑知识迁移、又能捕捉建筑个性化时序模式的适配方案。

Method: 提出个性化联邦稀疏适配框架，核心是在预训练TSFM表示层之后放置一个异质时序混合专家（MoE）适配器。该适配器包含多个专家，分别专长于周期性、长程交互、局部变化、趋势-残差结构及多分辨率行为。序列级路由器将每个168小时上下文窗口映射到一个top-k专家子集。在联邦学习框架下，对比了全局共享专家库与客户端私有专家库的多种变体，包括Global FL-MoE、Local MoE及个性化FL-MoE。

Result: 在50栋建筑、三种TSFM骨干上的实验表明：(1)个性化策略一致优于Global FL-MoE和Local MoE；(2)最优稀疏适配策略因骨干和评估指标而异；(3)路由行为分析揭示了客户端级别的专家专长分化、专家集中度差异，以及跨骨干的近似均匀路由模式，表明联邦TSFM适配应同时考虑客户端感知和骨干感知。

Conclusion: 联邦时序基础模型适配不应采用"一刀切"的参数共享策略。本文证明了个性化联邦稀疏适配结合异质MoE的有效性，同时揭示最优适配策略依赖于具体的TSFM骨干和评价指标，强调联邦TSFM适配需要兼顾客户端感知与骨干感知的设计原则。

Abstract: Federated adaptation of time-series foundation models (TSFMs) is attractive for building energy forecasting because meter data are private, distributed, and highly non-IID. However, a single parameter-sharing strategy is unlikely to serve all pretrained TSFMs or building clients: fully shared adapters can suppress building-specific temporal behavior, while fully local adaptation discards cross-building transfer. We propose a personalized federated sparse adaptation framework with a heterogeneous temporal mixture-of-experts (MoE) adapter placed after the pretrained TSFM representation. A sequence-level router maps each 168-hour context window to a top-$k$ subset of experts specialized for periodicity, long-range interactions, local variation, trend-residual structure, and multi-resolution behavior. We compare global FL, local training, and personalized FL variants with globally shared or client-private expert banks. Across 50 buildings and three TSFM backbones, personalization consistently outperforms Global FL-MoE and Local MoE, while the best sparse-adaptation strategy varies by backbone and metric. Routing behavior further reveals client-level expert specialization, expert concentration, and near-uniform routing across backbones, showing that federated TSFM adaptation should be both client-aware and backbone-aware.

</details>


### [248] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecDrop 是一种无参数的固定路由方案，通过为每个分支分配类别专属权重和微小泄漏权重，无需学习路由器或辅助损失函数，在需要类别标签的推理场景下，证明路由机制的有效性取决于训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐，而非路由算法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MoE网络中，学习到的路由器在同等参数量预算下往往不如等权重的无路由基线。研究者试图探究瓶颈究竟在于路由算法本身，还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的错位。

Method: 提出SpecDrop：一种固定的无参数路由方案，K个分支中每个分支对其分配类别获得权重p_a，对其他类别有微小泄漏p_i>0，通过类别无关的固定分母合并，无学习路由参数和辅助损失。推理时需要类别标签。实验覆盖CIFAR-100（ResNet-110）、ImageNet-1K（ViT-S/16）的图像分类任务，以及SlimPajama-6B语言建模和SuperNI指令微调等模糊分区任务。

Result: CIFAR-100上达79.23%，超密集基线+4.75；ImageNet-1K上达79.89%，超No-Routing+SE基线+6.53。分支-类别对齐达58%/100%，masking增益仅0.00/1.06，表明输出空间限制在训练中被内化。在模糊分区任务（语言建模、指令微调）中，路由退化为与无路由基线无显著差异，验证了粒度对齐而非算法选择是路由有效的关键。

Conclusion: 路由机制是否有效取决于训练信号粒度与目标类别的对齐程度，而非路由算法的选择。当粒度与类别对齐时，固定无参数路由也能超越学习型路由器；当粒度模糊时，路由无益。SpecDrop将类别标签转化为训练內化的模块化结构。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks pursue specialization through learned routers, gates, and load-balancing losses, yet at matched total-parameter budgets learned routers can underperform equal-weight No-Routing baselines. Is the bottleneck the routing algorithm, or the alignment between training-signal granularity and the target categories? We probe the question with SpecDrop, a fixed parameter-free routing scheme: each of $K$ branches receives weight $p_a$ for its assigned category and a small leakage $p_i > 0$ otherwise, merged through a category-independent fixed denominator, with no learned routing parameters and no auxiliary losses; the category label is required at inference. On vision tasks where each image has one superclass label (CIFAR-100 on ResNet-110; ImageNet-1K on ViT-S/16), SpecDrop reaches 79.23% on CIFAR-100 and 79.89% on ImageNet-1K, exceeding parameter-matched baselines that do not use the label (+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100; +6.53 over the No-Routing+SE control on ImageNet-1K). These gains quantify what category supervision buys when deployed through routing -- not an advantage over label-aware deployments of the baselines: given the same label, masking a dense model's outputs is stronger for accuracy alone (85.2 / 83.7). SpecDrop's contribution is converting the label into trained-in modular structure: 58%/100% branch-category alignment, and masking gains of 0.00 (CIFAR) / +1.06 (ImageNet) -- the output-space restriction is largely internalized during training. On fuzzy partitions, where training units span multiple categories (SlimPajama-6B language modeling with a 30M Transformer; SuperNI instruction tuning over Llama-3.2-1B with LoRA), the routing mechanism reduces to the matched No-Routing controls within seed noise, the null our thesis predicts. Granularity alignment, not algorithm choice, localizes when routing helps. Code: https://github.com/Beryex/SpecDrop

</details>


### [249] [Out-Of-The-Loop Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04113)
*Gustavo Sutter,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多保真贝叶斯优化中最高保真度函数过于昂贵而无法参与优化循环的现实场景，本文指出标准MF-BO算法的次优性，并提出利用历史高保真数据及任务描述符来改进优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际MF-BO任务中，最高保真度函数过于昂贵，无法在优化循环中被查询。然而，从业者往往拥有来自先前实验的"金标准"数据，标准MF-BO算法即便在理想假设下也无法有效利用这些历史高保真数据，导致次优性能。

Method: 将历史高保真数据与任务描述符（可显式给出或从非结构化元数据中提取）纳入MF-BO框架，利用跨任务的信息共享来指导当前优化过程。

Result: 在合成函数、化学分子优化以及超参数优化等真实世界问题上验证了方法的有效性，相比标准MF-BO算法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 标准MF-BO算法在存在历史高保真数据的场景下表现次优；通过引入任务描述符来利用历史数据，可以显著提升多保真贝叶斯优化的实际效果。

Abstract: Black-box optimization is a ubiquitous problem in science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions with cheaper lower-fidelity proxies available. Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MF-BO) is a principled approach to this problem, leveraging correlations across different fidelities when querying the objective. However, for many important MF-BO tasks, the true highest-fidelity function is prohibitively expensive to be part of the optimization loop. Nevertheless, practitioners often have gold standard data (observations of the highest-fidelity function) obtained from previous experiments that might provide information for the current task. For instance, in molecular optimization, chemists often pick the top-$k$ candidate molecules using various computer simulations, and later reveal their true objective function values. In this work, we demonstrate the suboptimality of standard MF-BO algorithms in the real-world scenarios above, even under ideal assumptions. Next, we mitigate this problem by incorporating historical high-fidelity data accompanied by task descriptors---which can be explicitly given or extracted from unstructured metadata. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on synthetic functions, as well as real-world problems in chemistry and hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [250] [Variational Bounds for Perceptron Learning from Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04882)
*Francesco Camilli,Pierluigi Contucci,Federica Gerace,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限温度下连续自旋感知器的变分方法，通过插值法与对数凹性、浓度估计相结合，推导出淬火压力的极小极大变分上下界。两界仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上存在差异，当优化可交换时两界匹配，从而精确求解模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的感知器模型分析主要集中在零温或离散自旋情形，缺乏对有限温度、连续自旋及广泛凹效用函数和先验分布的统一变分框架。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一条统一的计算路径来获得基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差。

Method: 作者将插值方法（interpolation method）与对数凹性（log-concavity）和浓度估计（concentration estimates）相结合，针对高斯混合数据训练的连续自旋感知器，在允许凹效用函数和对数凹可分离先验测度的设定下，推导出淬火压力（quenched pressure）的变分极小极大上下界，并通过变分势的凹-凸结构控制极值条件。

Result: 推导出的下界和上界仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上不同，其余所有极值均由变分势的凹-凸结构决定。当两个优化可交换时，上下界匹配，从而精确确定模型的解。该变分势还以驻点条件的形式给出不动点方程，并统一给出基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差的计算方法。

Conclusion: 该变分框架为有限温度连续自旋感知器模型提供了严格且统一的解析工具，极小极大变分界的紧致性条件由优化可交换性刻画，为理解此类模型的统计力学性质和泛化行为开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce a variational approach to a finite-temperature continuous-spin perceptron trained on a Gaussian mixture. The model allows for a broad class of concave utilities and log-concave separable prior measures on the spins. By combining the interpolation method with log-concavity and concentration estimates, we derive lower and upper minimax variational bounds for the limiting quenched pressure. Remarkably, the two bounds differ only in the order of optimization of two variational parameters, while all remaining extrema are controlled by the concave--convex structure of the variational potential. Whenever the two optimizations commute, the two bounds match and identify the solution of the model. The same potential yields the fixed-point equations as stationarity conditions and provides a unified route to the computation of the ground-state energy, training loss, and generalization error.

</details>


### [251] [LiNC: Lightweight Noise Correction via Per-Sample Trust and Gaussian Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04147)
*Abhishek Moturu,Babak Taati,Anna Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiNC 为每个训练样本引入一个可学习的信任参数，通过原始标签与模型预测的凸组合进行训练，利用信任值的高斯混合模型分离干净/模糊/噪声样本，再执行软硬校正，在最高50%标签噪声下实现稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据集中因评分者间变异性、标注错误和模糊病例等因素导致标签噪声普遍存在，严重损害机器学习模型的可靠性和临床有效性，因此需要一种轻量且有效的噪声校正方法。

Method: 提出 LiNC（轻量噪声校正）方法：(1) 为每个训练样本附加一个可学习的信任参数，目标函数为观测标签与模型预测分布的凸组合；(2) 利用早期训练中干净/噪声样本信任值梯度方向相反的特性，获得可分离的信任分布；(3) 使用三组分高斯混合模型（GMM）将信任值分为干净、模糊、噪声三类；(4) 对噪声样本先进行软校正阶段，再进行最终的硬校正阶段。

Result: 在 MedMNISTv2 的十个 2D 数据集上，于高达 50% 标签噪声条件下，LiNC 在精度上实现了一致性提升，并展现出强大的误标签检测能力。训练时间复杂度仍由基础网络主导，额外内存开销仅随训练集规模线性增长，渐近开销可忽略。

Conclusion: LiNC 以极小的额外计算和内存开销，有效解决了医学影像中的标签噪声问题，在多种噪声比例下均表现稳健，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Label noise is common in medical imaging datasets due to factors such as inter-rater variability, annotation errors, and ambiguous cases. This can severely undermine the reliability and clinical effectiveness of machine learning models trained using those datasets. To address this challenge, we introduce Lightweight Noise Correction (LiNC), which adds a single trainable trust parameter per training sample and learns when to use the observed label and when to defer to the model during a standard training loop. The key idea is to train using a convex combination of the observed label and the model's own predictive distribution, controlled by a per-sample trust parameter. We show that the gradient of this objective drives trust values in opposite directions for clean versus noisy samples in the early training phase, yielding separable trust distributions. We use a 3-component Gaussian Mixture Model over the trust values to separate them into clean, ambiguous, and noisy cases and then execute a short soft-correction phase on the noisy cases and a final hard correction phase. Experiments on ten 2D datasets from MedMNISTv2 under label noise of up to 50% show consistent gains in accuracy and strong mislabel detection. LiNC adds negligible asymptotic overhead: the training-time complexity remains dominated by the base network, with additional memory growing linearly with the size of the training set.

</details>


### [252] [Stochastic Emulation using Generalized Stratified Sampling for Performance-Based Risk Optimization of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05006)
*Isabela D. Rodrigues,Seymour M. J. Spence,Henrique M. Kroetz,André T. Beck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将广义分层抽样(GSS)与随机多项式混沌展开(SPCE)结合的框架，以改善结构响应分布尾部极端响应的建模精度，并将其应用于屈曲约束支撑钢框架的基于性能的风险优化(PBRO)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPCE在近似结构响应分布时无需在固定输入条件下重复进行非线性分析，但在准确表达响应分布尾部极端响应方面存在局限性，这对可靠度分析和基于性能的风险优化至关重要。

Method: 提出GSS-SPCE框架：按灾害强度将输入空间划分为多个层(strata)，每层内独立训练SPCE仿真器；各层估计的条件超越概率通过全概率定理重新组合以评估概率约束。

Result: 在一栋两层钢框架建筑的屈曲约束支撑截面优化设计中验证了框架的有效性，结果表明该框架能准确估计结构响应分布（包括尾部区域），同时大幅减少PBRO所需的非线性模型评估次数。

Conclusion: GSS-SPCE框架有效克服了SPCE在尾部响应建模中的不足，在保证精度的同时显著降低了计算成本，为结构PBRO提供了一种高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Metamodels are instrumental in reducing the computational burden associated with nested reliability analyses and optimization loops in Performance-Based Risk Optimization (PBRO) of structures under stochastic loads. In this context, stochastic emulators are particularly useful because they approximate response distributions while accounting for the intrinsic stochasticity of the simulator. Among these methods, Stochastic Polynomial Chaos Expansion (SPCE) is especially attractive because it does not require replications of nonlinear analyses at fixed input conditions. However, SPCE may present limitations in accurately representing extreme responses in the tails of structural response distributions. To address this limitation, this study proposes a framework that combines Generalized Stratified Sampling (GSS) with SPCE. The GSS scheme partitions the input space into strata according to the intensity of the hazard, improving the representation of extreme responses, while independent SPCE emulators are trained within each stratum. The conditional exceedance probabilities estimated in each stratum are then recombined using the total probability theorem to evaluate the probabilistic constraints. The proposed GSS-SPCE framework is applied to the optimal design of buckling-restrained brace cross-sectional areas in a two-story steel building. The objective is to minimize the initial construction cost while satisfying prescribed probabilistic performance constraints. Results show that the proposed framework accurately estimates structural response distributions, including their tail regions, while substantially reducing the number of nonlinear model evaluations required for PBRO.

</details>


### [253] [MINT: Tensor Decomposition on Stacked Recurrence Matrices for Time Series Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04157)
*Kaamil Kaka,Audrey Der,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Zachary Zimmerman,Vikram Jayaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MINT（矩阵型非负张量分解）管道，将递归图扩展为张量化的自相似性矩阵，对多时间序列数据集进行联合模式挖掘与共聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统递归图仅针对单条时间序列，缺乏对多条时间序列数据集进行联合分析和跨序列共聚类模式挖掘的有效原语。需要一种能够自然扩展到多变量场景、并可利用张量分解进行协同模式发现的新原语。

Method: 提出张量化自相似性矩阵原语：对包含N条长度为n的时间序列数据集，以长度为m的子序列窗口计算规模为N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)的点图（dot plot）张量；随后采用张量分解方法（特别是非负张量分解）对该三阶张量进行挖掘，实现跨序列的共聚类模式发现，整体流程称为MINT管道。

Result: 在轨道交通、电力需求、风力涡轮机和汽车交通四个真实数据集上验证，MINT管道能够有效共聚类具有规律性间隔的重复模式（motif）的跨传感器模式，尤其适用于高度规整的数据集。

Conclusion: 张量化自相似性矩阵是时间序列数据挖掘的有效原语，结合张量分解能够成功发现多时间序列间的共聚类结构，在规律性强的领域数据中表现突出。

Abstract: Recurrence plots are a time series data mining primitive applied to a variety of domains (e.g. star light curves, sound waveforms, CCT telemetry). This work proposes tensorized self-similarity matrices as a primitive for univariate time series datasets ($N\times n$) of $N$ time series of length $n$ with a subsequence window of length $m$, and whose tensor-based nature is naturally extensible to multivariate datasets. The proposed method to compute this primitive computes dot plots of size $N \times (n-m+1) \times (n-m+ 1)$ from these datasets, where the subsequent tensor is mined using tensor decomposition methods to mine for co-clustered patterns. We demonstrate our results in mass rapid transit, electricity demand, wind turbine, and car traffic data, finding the MINT pipeline effectively co-clusters cross-sensor patterns in highly regular datasets containing motifs at regular intervals.

</details>


### [254] [Canonical Joint Energy-Based Model on CIFAR-10: failure modes and practical indistinguishability of Predictor-Corrector and SGLD samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05025)
*Dmytro Knopov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对联合能量模型（JEM）中Predictor-Corrector（PC）采样器与标准SGLD进行了系统性复现对比。在WideResNet-28-10上两次独立运行发现，PC在无退火噪声调度下并未展现理论优势：分类精度、FID和OOD检测AUROC三项指标上两种方法实际表现不可区分，且论文记录了训练后期灾难性发散这一共同失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典JEM训练使用SGLD，而PC采样器在理论上具备更优的采样保证。然而，PC从未在经典JEM模型上经过系统性复现检验。作者旨在验证：在无退火噪声调度的实际训练条件下，PC是否能将其理论优势转化为经验性能提升。

Method: 在无归一化层的WideResNet-28-10上复现经典JEM（两次独立运行），对比三种协议：(1) PC完全替代SGLD完成约130个epoch训练；(2) 冷启动生成评估（FID）；(3) 精炼式多OOD检测（AUROC）。统计分析采用分层seed-by-image bootstrap构建AUROC差异95%置信区间，以及基于每方法两次运行的seed-level等效性检验。

Result: 复现达到92.88%测试精度和44.46 buffer-FID（经典结果为92.9%和38.40）。两种方法在所有协议上表现不可区分：推理阶段十个checkpoint-OOD对的AUROC绝对差异均低于0.007，FID差异低于0.5；宏观平均AUROC差异的95% bootstrap置信区间包含零，但等效性检验因样本量不足无法确认形式等效。记录了两个失效模式：经典离群值缓冲机制导致的训练后期灾难性发散（SGLD和PC均出现），以及依赖运行种子的SVHN OOD判别动态。

Conclusion: PC在无退火噪声条件下并未为经典JEM带来理论预期优势，实际表现与SGLD无实质区别。这一结果在理论上是可预期的：固定噪声下PC的预测步因构造退化为无效，其理论保证无法迁移至经典JEM设置。数据既与等效性一致，也不排除存在小幅方向性效应。

Abstract: Joint Energy-Based Models (JEM) unify classification and generation within a single network and support out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. Canonical JEM training relies on stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD); a theoretically motivated alternative, the Predictor-Corrector (PC) sampler, has not previously undergone a systematic replication test on the canonical model. We reproduce canonical JEM on WideResNet-28-10 without normalisation layers on two independent runs and test whether PC retains its theoretical advantage without an annealed noise schedule, across three protocols: PC replacing SGLD throughout the roughly 130 training epochs; cold-start generation (FID); and refinement-style multi-OOD detection (AUROC). The reconstruction reaches 92.88% test accuracy and buffer-FID 44.46 (canonical: 92.9% and 38.40). We document two failure modes: catastrophic late-training divergence via the canonical outlier-buffer mechanism (both SGLD runs and, with the same signature, both PC runs), and run-dependent SVHN OOD-discrimination dynamics. No method-level advantage of PC over SGLD is observed on any protocol: at inference the absolute AUROC difference stays below 0.007 across all ten checkpoint-OOD pairs and the FID difference below 0.5; on the training protocol a hierarchical seed-by-image bootstrap gives a 95% confidence interval on the macro-averaged AUROC difference that contains zero, while a seed-level equivalence test with two runs per method cannot establish formal equivalence. The data are consistent both with equivalence and with a small directional effect. This practical indistinguishability is theoretically expected: under fixed noise the PC predictor step degenerates by construction, so its guarantees do not transfer to canonical JEM.

</details>


### [255] [Understanding Fault Tolerance of Adversarially Robust Pruned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04173)
*Manali Dangarikar,Cory Merkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了剪枝、对抗训练与硬件故障注入三者对卷积神经网络鲁棒性的交互影响，发现对抗训练虽提升输入扰动鲁棒性，却增加了对硬件权重故障的敏感性，而剪枝对故障敏感性影响不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在资源受限神经形态硬件上的DNN同时面临剪枝压缩、对抗攻击和硬件故障三重挑战，但三者的联合效应鲜有研究，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 使用MNIST上训练的三层紧凑CNN，设计三组实验：(1)比较自然训练与对抗训练模型在同时遭受硬件故障和对抗攻击时的容错性；(2)评估剪枝对对抗鲁棒性的影响；(3)刻画故障率、对抗扰动幅度和剪枝水平三者构成的联合精度曲面。

Result: 对抗训练提升了对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但增加了对stuck-at-zero权重故障的敏感性；剪枝并未显著增加故障敏感性，且不同剪枝水平在各类故障率和攻击强度下影响均很小。

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒性与硬件可靠性需要联合考量，单一维度的优化可能在另一维度引入脆弱性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) deployed on resource-constrained neuromorphic hardware face three concurrent challenges: the need for model compression through pruning, vulnerability to adversarial input perturbations, and susceptibility to hardware-induced weight faults such as stuck-at-zero errors. While each of these factors has been studied in isolation, their combined effects on model reliability have received little attention. This paper presents an empirical investigation of how pruning, adversarial training, and hardware fault injection interact to affect the robustness of convolutional neural networks. Using a compact three-layer CNN trained on MNIST, we conduct three experiments: (1) comparing the fault tolerance of naturally and adversarially trained models under simultaneous hardware faults and adversarial attacks, (2) evaluating how pruning affects adversarial robustness, and (3) characterizing the joint accuracy surface across fault rates, adversarial perturbation magnitudes, and pruning levels. Our results show that adversarial training improves robustness against input perturbations but increases sensitivity to stuck-at-zero weight faults. Contrary to intuition, pruning did not significantly increase fault sensitivity, and varying the pruning level had little effect across fault rates and attack strengths. These results highlight the need to jointly consider adversarial robustness and hardware reliability.

</details>


### [256] [SSTQ:Privacy-Preserving Vector Quantization via Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05127)
*Adel Javanmard,David P. Woodruff,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SSTQ框架，结合过完备等范紧框架、坐标子采样和隐私感知一维量化，在分布式优化中以低通信代价实现局部差分隐私，相比vqSGD等方法显著降低方差并优化均方误差缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化中的局部差分隐私方法（如vqSGD）采用高维几何构造，导致方差与维度相关且不理想；同时需要在隐私保护与低通信成本之间取得更好的平衡。

Method: 提出Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ)框架，核心包含三个组件：(1) 过完备等范紧框架（overcomplete equal-norm tight frames）；(2) 坐标子采样（coordinate subsampling）；(3) 隐私感知一维量化。包含两种变体：Flat Randomized Response版本和Metric-Aware Laplace版本，后者更适合高码本位宽场景。此外，推导了代理隐私感知码本目标，将码本相关MSE缩放从O(4^b)降至O(2^b)。

Result: SSTQ在仅使用⌈log₂ N⌉ + b比特/客户端（N=Θ(d)为帧大小）的条件下，实现了最优均方误差缩放。在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST联邦学习任务上，相比现有基线方法展现出更优的效用和通信效率。

Conclusion: SSTQ框架成功解决了分布式优化中局部差分隐私与低通信成本难以兼顾的问题，通过维度无关的方差控制和优化的码本设计，在理论和实验上均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Achieving local differential privacy in distributed optimization while maintaining low communication cost remains challenging. Existing vector quantization methods, such as vqSGD, use high-dimensional geometric constructions but incur unfavorable dimension-dependent variance. In this work, we propose Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ), a framework that combines overcomplete equal-norm tight frames, coordinate subsampling, and privacy-aware one-dimensional quantization. SSTQ includes two variants: a Flat Randomized Response version and a Metric-Aware Laplace version, the latter being better suited to higher codebook bit-width regimes. We show that SSTQ achieves optimal mean squared error scaling while using only $\lceil \log_2 N \rceil + b$ bits per client, where $N = Θ(d)$ is the frame size. We also derive a surrogate privacy-aware codebook objective that reduces the codebook-dependent MSE scaling from $O(4^b)$ to $O(2^b)$. Finally, we empirically evaluate SSTQ against established baselines on federated learning tasks using CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, demonstrating favorable utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [257] [TS2TabPFN: Time Series Classification and Extrinsic Regression through Feature Extraction and a Tabular Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04174)
*Gabriel da Costa Merlin,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TS2TabPFN 将显式特征提取与表格基础模型 TabPFN 2.5 相结合，在时间序列分类和外在回归任务上显著超越现有最优方法，确立了新的性能标杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列方法存在两极化缺陷：基于特征工程的方法可控但性能受限，端到端深度学习模型自动化程度高但缺乏可解释性与可控性。该鸿沟亟需一种能融合两者优势的新框架。

Method: 提出 TS2TabPFN 框架：首先从时间序列中显式提取结构化特征，将其转化为表格数据格式，随后输入 TabPFN 2.5（面向表格数据的前沿基础模型）进行预测，从而将特征工程的可控性与基础模型的强大预测能力有机结合。

Result: 在时间序列外在回归（TSER）任务上，TS2TabPFN 以统计显著性超越现有最优模型；在时间序列分类（TSC）任务上也超越了大多数当前最佳算法，同时兼具鲁棒性和高效性。

Conclusion: 将基础模型与结构化特征相结合能够突破单一范式的局限，TS2TabPFN 由此确立了时间序列领域的新最优水平，为后续研究提供了融合范式的新方向。

Abstract: Time series data are ubiquitous in practical applications, where classification (TSC) and extrinsic regression (TSER) have emerged as essential tasks for obtaining value from temporal sequences. While the literature has seen significant progress through feature-based and deep learning models, existing methods often focus either on the quality of feature extraction or on the intrinsic predictive power of complex architectures applied to raw data. This division creates a gap between the control offered by feature engineering and the automated performance of end-to-end models. This paper proposes TS2TabPFN, a framework that bridges this gap by integrating explicit feature extraction with TabPFN 2.5, a cutting-edge foundation model for tabular data, to leverage its predictive capabilities. Our extensive experimental evaluation demonstrates that TS2TabPFN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art models in TSER tasks with statistical significance, providing a robust and efficient alternative for TSC and surpassing most of the currently best-performing algorithms. These results suggest that combining foundation models with structured features overcomes single-paradigm limitations, establishing a new time series state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [258] [A Comparative Study of Feature Selection Methods for EHR Diagnosis Codes in Opioid Use Disorder Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04180)
*Zihan Ding,Yinan Liu,Tengfei Ma,Rachel Wong,George Leibowitz,Benjamin Littenberg,Xia Zheng,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种特征选择方法在基于电子健康记录的阿片类药物使用障碍预测中的表现，发现NTK梯度敏感性方法在准确性与稳定性之间取得了最佳平衡，而LLM引导的选择虽单独表现较弱，但能提供互补的临床有意义信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR预测建模中特征通常高维、稀疏、噪声大且冗余，大特征集不仅增加计算负担和过拟合风险，还使模型难以解释，限制了临床实用性。因此需要系统评估不同特征选择范式，为临床预测建模提供指导。

Method: 以诊断相关特征为焦点，在统一预处理和评估框架下比较五种特征选择范式：(1) 复发富集分析；(2) 基于神经正切核(NTK)的早期梯度敏感性；(3) LightGBM-SHAP；(4) Elastic Net；(5) 大语言模型(LLM)引导的语义选择。评估维度包括下游预测性能、重采样稳定性和对低频诊断编码的代表性。

Result: 特征预算增大可提升性能，但在中等规模后收益递减；NTK敏感性方法在准确性和稳定性方面综合表现最优；LLM引导选择虽单独预测性能较低，但能贡献互补的临床有意义信号。

Conclusion: NTK早期梯度敏感性是OUD预测中兼顾准确性与稳定性的最佳特征选择方法，而LLM语义选择可作为补充手段引入临床领域知识。适中的特征规模即可实现接近最优的预测性能。

Abstract: Feature selection is a critical step in electronic health record (EHR)-based predictive modeling, where input variables are often high-dimensional, sparse, noisy, and redundant. Large feature sets not only increase computational burden and overfitting risk, but also make model interpretation difficult, leading to limited usefulness in clinical settings. In this study, we focus on diagnosis-related features and compare five feature selection paradigms for opioid use disorder (OUD) prediction: recurrence enrichment, NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity, LightGBM-SHAP, Elastic Net, and large language model (LLM)-guided semantic selection. We use a unified preprocessing and evaluation framework and assess each method by downstream predictive performance, resampling stability, and representation of infrequent diagnosis codes. Our results demonstrate that performance improves with larger feature budgets with diminishing returns beyond a moderate size. NTK sensitivity provides the best overall balance of accuracy and stability, and LLM-guided selection contributes complementary clinically meaningful signals despite lower standalone performance.

</details>


### [259] [Unscented KalmanNet: a hybrid deep learning filter with calibrated posterior covariance for nonlinear state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04201)
*Minhyeok Ko,Abdollah Shafieezadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UKN 将 UKF 与两个可学习模块（NoiseNet 和 GainNet）相结合，前者预测时变噪声协方差的有界乘性修正，后者对解析增益施加残差校正，并采用校准感知的联合训练目标，在合成和真实飞行数据上均显著优于 UKF、KalmanNet 和 Bayesian KalmanNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，未知且时变的噪声统计特性以及模型失配会严重损害 UKF 的估计精度和协方差校准质量；现有基于学习的滤波器多构建于 EKF 之上，要么放弃显式协方差，要么未针对失配引起的增益偏差进行校正。

Method: 提出 Unscented KalmanNet (UKN)：① NoiseNet 预测过程噪声和测量噪声协方差的有界乘性修正（相对于固定基线），保证正定性；② GainNet 对 UKF 解析增益施加有界残差校正；③ 训练目标采用自适应加权的校准感知损失，联合优化状态误差、协方差一致性和新息一致性。

Result: 在三个合成系统及 UZH-FPV 真实飞行数据上，UKN 均取得最低的聚合状态估计误差。合成案例中 RMSE 较 UKF 降低 26.4%-49.7%；11 次飞行留一序列交叉验证中，位置和速度 RMSE 分别降低 22.4% 和 34.3%，且归一化 NEES 和经验覆盖率最接近标称值，fold 间变异性最低。

Conclusion: UKN 通过结构化混合设计，在保留 UKF sigma 点协方差递推的同时，有效补偿噪声失配和模型偏差，在估计精度和校准一致性两个维度上均实现了显著且稳健的提升。

Abstract: State estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems is commonly performed with the Unscented Kalman filter (UKF), which propagates the state moments through deterministic sigma points and reports a posterior covariance at every step. In practice, however, unknown and time-varying noise statistics and model mismatch degrade both estimation accuracy and covariance calibration. Existing learned filters improve accuracy but are largely built on the extended Kalman filter and either forgo an explicit covariance or learn uncertainty without correcting mismatch-induced gain bias. This paper introduces the Unscented KalmanNet (UKN), a hybrid recursive estimator that augments the UKF with two structurally distinct learned components while preserving its explicit sigma-point covariance recursion. NoiseNet predicts time-varying process and measurement covariances as bounded multiplicative corrections to fixed baselines, guaranteeing positive definiteness, while GainNet applies a bounded residual correction to the analytical gain. A calibration-aware training objective combines state error with covariance- and innovation-consistency terms through adaptive weights, jointly optimizing accuracy and calibration. UKN is benchmarked against UKF, KalmanNet, and Bayesian KalmanNet on three synthetic systems and UZH-FPV real-flight data. It achieves the lowest aggregate state-estimation error in all four examples and reduces RMSE by $26.4$-$49.7\%$ compared to UKF in the synthetic cases. Leave-one-sequence-out cross-validation over 11 flights shows $22.4\%$ and $34.3\%$ reductions in mean position and velocity RMSE, respectively. UKN also yields the lowest fold-to-fold variability, with normalized NEES and empirical coverage closest to nominal values among the other filters.

</details>


### [260] [From Non-Convex Self-Concordant Regularization to Scalable Quasi-Newton Training of PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04206)
*Chenhao Si,Kang An,Shiqian Ma,Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCORE 是一种面向 PINN 训练的自协调启发式准牛顿方法，通过单一准牛顿减量联合确定强 Wolfe 检验的候选步长和自适应移位，用于构造下一步的割线几何，在多个 PDE 基准上优于 BFGS 和自缩放 Broyden 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 的残差目标函数常呈现不定、近奇异和尺度不良的局部曲率，导致高精度准牛顿精化困难；现有正则化准牛顿方法和自协调方法各自提供了稳定化和曲率依赖步长选择的机制，但缺乏将两者统一用于 PINN 训练的方案。

Method: 提出 SCORE 方法，核心机制是：从学习的逆度量计算单一准牛顿减量，该减量同时决定一个经强 Wolfe 条件检验的候选步长和一个自适应移位，用于定义下一步割线几何。移位位移表示沿接受步长的平均移位度量作用，无需 Hessian 构造或 Hessian-向量积。全局化通过强 Wolfe 接受、回退线搜索和标准曲率保护实现，不修改底层 PINN 目标函数。理论分析表明，在局部谱等价条件下，准牛顿减量和候选步长与正移位度量中的对应量可比，并在匹配度量情形恢复归一化自协调规则。

Result: 在 viscous Burgers、Kuramoto–Sivashinsky、Korteweg–de Vries 和 complex Ginzburg–Landau 方程上的实验表明，SCORE 的最终误差低于 BFGS 和自缩放 Broyden 基线。Burgers 消融实验进一步表明，移位曲率稳定化和基于减量的步长选择对高精度精化具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: SCORE 将自协调规则与移位割线几何统一在准牛顿减量框架中，为 PINN 训练提供了一种无需 Hessian 访问的稳定高精度优化方法，在多个 PDE 问题上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often require high-accuracy quasi-Newton refinement to obtain reliable partial differential equation solutions, but their residual objectives can exhibit indefinite, nearly singular, and poorly scaled local curvature. Regularized quasi-Newton methods provide established mechanisms for stabilizing secant models, while self-concordant methods provide local-metric rules for curvature-dependent step selection. Building on these two lines of work, we propose SCORE, a self-concordance-inspired quasi-Newton method with decrement-coupled shifted secant geometry for PINN training. Its distinguishing mechanism is that a single quasi-Newton decrement computed from the learned inverse metric jointly determines a strong-Wolfe-tested candidate step and an adaptive shift used to define the next secant geometry. The shifted displacement represents the action of an averaged shifted metric along the accepted step, while requiring neither Hessian construction nor Hessian-vector products. Under a local spectral-equivalence condition, we show that the quasi-Newton decrement and candidate step remain comparable to their counterparts in a positive shifted metric, and recover the normalized self-concordant rule in the matched-metric case. Strong Wolfe acceptance, fallback line search, and standard curvature safeguards provide globalization without modifying the underlying PINN objective. Experiments on the viscous Burgers, Kuramoto--Sivashinsky, Korteweg--de Vries, and complex Ginzburg--Landau equations show that SCORE attains lower final errors than the tested BFGS and self-scaled Broyden baselines. The Burgers ablation further indicates that shifted curvature stabilization and decrement-based step selection make complementary contributions to high-accuracy refinement.

</details>


### [261] [Attention-Only White-Box Transformer via LeJEPA-Based Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04213)
*Yang Bai,Linyuan Wang,Haoyang Jiang,Nuolin Sun,Libin Hou,Bin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从联合视角重新审视自监督学习范式与白盒网络优化，发现LeJEPA框架中的各向同性高斯假设与稀疏率缩减目标中的展开项R(Z)等价，进而利用ADMM将剩余项推导为纯注意力Transformer（去除ISTA/MLP），在CIFAR上以约31%参数削减实现竞争性能，并在标准ViT上发现MLP模块存在显著冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白盒网络的自监督学习研究通常将网络推导（优化算法层面）与自监督学习范式割裂对待，缺乏统一的联合视角。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，探索两者之间的内在联系，并简化白盒Transformer结构。

Method: 1. 揭示LeJEPA自监督框架中各向同性高斯先验与稀疏率缩减目标中R(Z)展开项的等价性；2. 利用LeJEPA范式优化R(Z)；3. 通过ADMM将剩余项R^c(Z|U_{[K]})+λ||Z||_0推导为纯注意力Transformer，去除原CRATE中的ISTA结构和MLP层；4. 进一步在标准ViT上验证MLP冗余性，使用ReLU激活+知识蒸馏替换MLP块。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100上，纯注意力白盒Transformer（Base规模）分类准确率分别为88.88%和63.54%，与CRATE（89.18%/63.56%）几乎持平，但参数减少约31%。在标准ViT上，用ReLU+知识蒸馏替换所有MLP块可移除约66%参数，且保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 自监督学习范式与白盒网络优化目标之间存在深刻的等价关系，可据此大幅简化网络结构。MLP模块在白盒乃至标准ViT中均存在显著冗余，为未来ViT架构的精简设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Existing studies on self-supervised learning for white-box networks typically decouple the derivation of white-box networks via optimization algorithms from self-supervised learning paradigms. In this work, we instead revisit the two components from a joint perspective. The LeJEPA-based self-supervised framework assumes an isotropic Gaussian distribution as the optimal embedding distribution for downstream tasks, which is conceptually equivalent to the expansion term $R(Z)$ in the sparse rate reduction objective guiding white-box Transformer optimization. Building on this observation, we use the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm to optimize $R(Z)$, and derive the remaining terms $R^{c}(Z\mid U_{[K]})+λ\lVert Z\rVert_{0}$ via the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) into an attention-only Transformer that dispenses with the ISTA structure or MLP layers of the original design. Experimental results demonstrate that our attention-only white-box Transformer achieves classification accuracies of $88.88\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.54\%$ on CIFAR-100 at the Base scale under the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm, while the original white-box Transformer CRATE achieves classification accuracies of $89.18\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.56\%$ on CIFAR-100. Our model achieves competitive performance while reducing the parameter count by roughly $31\%$. Beyond the white-box setting, we further investigate standard ViTs and find that replacing all MLP blocks with ReLU activations under knowledge distillation removes approximately 66\% of the parameters while preserving competitive accuracy, motivating further investigation into the potential redundancy of MLP modules in standard ViT architectures.

</details>


### [262] [Random features for Grassmannian kernel approximation with bounded rank-one projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04227)
*Rémi Delogne,Laurent Jacques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一族基于秩一投影的随机特征映射方法，用于在Grassmann流形上高效近似旋转不变的Grassmannian核函数，显著降低大规模高维子空间数据集的计算和存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Grassmannian核（如投影核和Binet-Cauchy核）需要计算完整的Gram矩阵，对于大规模高维子空间数据集而言，计算和内存代价过高。需要一种可扩展的近似方案。

Method: 通过秩一投影作用于子空间投影矩阵，再施加有界非线性变换（周期变换或二值变换），构造随机特征。周期变换对应的近似核具有闭式表达，可在逆Binet-Cauchy与高斯型核之间调节；二值变换产生紧凑的1-bit子空间特征。进一步利用基于快速傅里叶变换的结构化秩一投影加速计算。

Result: 当特征数相对子空间内蕴维度足够大时，随机特征空间中的内积以高概率在所有固定维子空间上一致逼近明确定义的旋转不变Grassmannian核。合成数据与ETH-80分类实验表明，该方法在准确保持Grassmannian几何结构的同时，显著降低了计算、内存和存储开销。

Conclusion: 秩一嵌入为经典Grassmannian核提供了一种实用且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose a family of random feature maps for scalable kernel machines on low-dimensional subspaces, ie on the Grassmannian manifold. Such representations are useful when data classes or clusters are well described by the span of a few samples. Classical Grassmannian kernels, including the projection and Binet-Cauchy kernels, require full Gram matrices, which leads to prohibitive computational and memory costs for large high-dimensional subspace datasets. We address this limitation using random features based on rank-one projections of subspace projection matrices followed by bounded non-linear transforms, either periodic or binary, to control the resulting distributions.
  We show that inner products in the random feature space approximate well-defined rotation-invariant Grassmannian kernels that depend only on the principal angles between subspaces. When the number of features is sufficiently large relative to the intrinsic subspace dimension, the approximation holds uniformly over all fixed-dimensional subspaces with high probability. For periodic transforms, the approximated kernel has a closed-form expression with tunable behaviour between inverse Binet-Cauchy and Gaussian-type regimes. Binary transforms yield compact one-bit subspace features, although no closed-form kernel is known. Structured rank-one projections based on randomised fast Fourier transforms further reduce computation without sacrificing practical accuracy. Experiments on synthetic data and ETH-80 classification tasks show that these features accurately preserve Grassmannian geometry while reducing computation, memory, and storage. Rank-one embeddings therefore provide a practical and scalable alternative to classical Grassmannian kernels.

</details>


### [263] [Transferable Dual-Stream Representations for Mesoscale-Preserving Sea Surface Temperature Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04230)
*Parth Doshi,Priyanka Aravindan,Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EddyFlow 是一个面向公里级海表温度降尺度的物理信息表示学习框架，在保持光谱保真度和跨区域泛化能力的同时，将零样本 RMSE 降低 21%，并在未见域上相对持续性预报达到最高 85.6% 的技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学时空降尺度深度学习方法通常仅最小化重建误差，忽略了物理上重要的多尺度结构，导致海表温度预测结果数值可信但过于平滑，丢失了对区域海洋动力学至关重要的中尺度变率。此外，现有方法多聚焦像素级目标或单一上下文条件，难以保持光谱保真度和跨区域泛化。

Method: EddyFlow 是一种表示学习框架，专为公里级海表温度降尺度设计，兼顾预测精度、尺度依赖结构和区域泛化能力。模型在圣劳伦斯湾训练，并在芬迪湾和墨西哥湾进行零样本与少样本评估。

Result: 物理信息表示学习使零样本 RMSE 降低 21%；在未见域上相对持续性预报的技巧最高达 85.6%；功率谱密度比值约为 1.00，实现了近理想的光谱保真度。

Conclusion: EddyFlow 有效解决了降尺度任务中光谱保真度不足和跨区域泛化困难的问题，通过物理信息表示学习在精度、结构保持和泛化性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Deep learning models for scientific spatio-temporal downscaling often minimize reconstruction error while failing to preserve physically meaningful multi-scale structure. For sea surface temperature prediction, this can yield outputs that are numerically plausible yet overly smooth, missing mesoscale variability critical to regional ocean dynamics. Existing methods often focus on pixel-wise objectives or single-context conditioning, which limits their ability to preserve spectral fidelity and generalize across regions. To address this, we propose EddyFlow, a representation learning framework for kilometer-scale sea surface temperature downscaling that balances predictive accuracy, scale-dependent structure, and regional generalization. EddyFlow is trained on the Gulf of St.~Lawrence and evaluated in zero-shot and few-shot settings on the Bay of Fundy and the Gulf of Mexico. EddyFlow demonstrates that physics-informed representation learning reduces zero-shot RMSE by 21%, achieves up to 85.6% skill relative to persistence on unseen domains, and maintains near-ideal spectral fidelity with a PSD ratio of $\approx 1.00$.

</details>


### [264] [Physics-informed reduced-order modelling with equivariant spectral submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04239)
*Georg Maierhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等变谱子流形(eSSM)方法，将全阶模型的对称性显式纳入SSM降阶过程，在加速计算的同时提升模型鲁棒性，并在多个基准测试上验证了优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱子流形(SSM)降阶虽在捕捉非线性动力学方面优于DMD等线性方法，但其计算成本极高，尤其对高维系统。现有SSM框架未充分利用全阶模型中固有的对称性来降低计算负担。

Method: 作者建立了eSSM的数学基础：证明SSM天然是等变子流形，且相关坐标卡与降阶动力学继承适当的诱导群作用。基于此框架，开发了一种等变SSM降阶算法，利用对称性加速计算并增强模型鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个基准问题上进行了验证，包括来自Common Task Framework for Science的测试，展示了该方法在计算速度和模型鲁棒性方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: eSSM将对称性理论与SSM降阶框架有机结合，为高维非线性系统的快速可靠降阶建模提供了新的原则性路径。

Abstract: Spectral submanifold (SSM) reduction has emerged as a mathematically principled route to reliable nonlinear reduced-order models, capturing dynamics beyond the reach of linear techniques such as Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD). The computation of SSMs, however, remains computationally expensive, particularly for high-dimensional systems. In this work, we introduce equivariant spectral submanifold (eSSM) reduction, a novel extension of the SSM framework that explicitly incorporates symmetries of the full-order model into the reduction process. We establish the mathematical foundations of this approach by showing that SSMs are naturally equivariant submanifolds and that the associated charts and reduced dynamics inherit the appropriate induced group actions. Building on this framework, we develop a novel equivariant SSM reduction algorithm that exploits these symmetries to achieve substantially faster computations while also improving model robustness. We demonstrate the advantages of this approach on several benchmark problems including a test from the Common Task Framework for Science.

</details>


### [265] [Attention-based representations for multi-task computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04243)
*Daniel Hsu,Mingyue Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多头注意力层中注意力头数量的表达能力下界，通过两个具体的多任务场景，建立了所需头数与嵌入维度/精度之间的理论权衡，并推广到任意对称布尔函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多头注意力机制的表达能力极限：在不同多任务场景下，究竟需要多少个注意力头？头数不足时是否可以通过增加嵌入维度或精度来补偿？

Method: 通过两个具体场景建立理论下界：(1) 同时计算列表中最小值和最大值——证明单头注意力需要指数级更大的嵌入维度或精度；(2) 计算n位XOR——建立头数与多项式次数的乘积下界为n，并构造匹配该下界的多头注意力层。进一步推广到任意对称布尔函数，用阈值度(threshold degree)刻画下界。

Result: (1) 最小/最大值场景：已知两个小嵌入维度、低精度的头即可完成任务，本文证明单个头需要指数级更大的嵌入维度或精度。(2) XOR场景：头数与多项式次数的乘积至少为n，并构造了匹配该下界的结构。结果推广到任意对称布尔函数，下界由阈值度给出。

Conclusion: 多头注意力层的表达能力存在严格的头数下界，头数不足无法简单地通过增加嵌入维度或精度来弥补，这为理解Transformer架构中多头机制的必要性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Multi-head attention layers produce vector representations that support multiple downstream tasks. We establish bounds on the number of heads required in two simple and concrete multi-task scenarios. In the first scenario, a vector representation is sought so that linear predictors can compute both the smallest and largest numbers in a given list. In this case, it is known two attention heads with small embedding dimension and bit precision level suffice. We prove that a single attention head requires exponentially higher embedding dimension or precision level. In the second scenario, a vector representation is sought so that a polynomial threshold function can compute the XOR of a given string of $n$ bits. This scenario is analogous to the first one for $n=2$, since XOR is readily computed by a linear function using a vector representation that encodes both the AND and the OR of the two bits. We observe that $n$-bit XOR requires the product of the number of heads and the polynomial degree to be at least $n$, and we construct multi-head attention layers that match this lower bound. These results generalize to arbitrary (symmetric) Boolean functions, where the bound is given in terms of the threshold degree.

</details>


### [266] [Geometry-Informed Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Pre-trained Molecular GNNs for Blood-Brain Barrier Permeability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04257)
*Marco Vieto Vega,Long D. Nguyen,Binh P. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BBBP-GeoPEFT，一种面向血脑屏障通透性预测的几何信息增强参数高效微调框架。该框架通过多截断距离图及其线图捕获三维空间原子相互作用和二阶边交互，结合节点级截断注意力与门控残差连接，在仅更新10.1%参数的情况下，达到与全量微调相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练分子GNN的全量微调参数效率低且易过拟合，而现有PEFT方法主要局限于节点特征或二维共价图的自适应，无法有效捕获三维几何构象和二阶相互作用，限制了其在BBBP这类对空间结构敏感任务上的表现。

Method: 提出BBBP-GeoPEFT框架：(1)从分子构象出发，构建多截断距离图及其对应的线图（line graph），分别捕获空间原子交互和二阶边-边交互；(2)使用轻量级辅助几何图编码器生成各截断特异性表示；(3)通过节点级截断注意力机制和门控残差连接，将几何表示逐层注入预训练GNN的各层，实现几何信息与预训练知识的融合，且仅引入少量可训练参数。

Result: 在 curated BBBP 数据集上，随机划分和骨架划分两种设置下，BBBP-GeoPEFT 在仅更新10.1%模型参数的情况下，在 ROC-AUC 和准确率等指标上达到与全量微调及代表性PEFT基线方法相当甚至更优的表现。

Conclusion: BBBP-GeoPEFT 验证了将三维几何信息与二阶交互通过PEFT方式融入预训练分子GNN的有效性，为参数受限场景下的分子性质预测任务提供了高效且性能优异的解决方案。

Abstract: Blood-brain barrier permeability (BBBP) prediction is a critical screening task in central nervous system drug discovery, where candidate molecules must be assessed for whether they can cross, or should be prevented from crossing, the blood-brain barrier. However, this task remains challenging because of limited, class-imbalanced datasets and sensitivity to molecular structure. Recent advances in deep learning have established graph neural networks (GNNs) as a powerful approach for molecular representation learning, while pre-trained molecular GNNs provide transferable knowledge for downstream tasks. However, full fine-tuning is often parameter-inefficient and prone to overfitting, whereas existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods mainly adapt node features or the two-dimensional covalent graph, limiting their ability to capture three-dimensional geometry and second-order interactions. To address these limitations, we propose BBBP-GeoPEFT, a geometry-informed PEFT framework for pre-trained molecular GNNs.
