Conclusion: 基于CSI的ISAC感知结合机器学习在铁路入侵检测中展现出高可靠性。该研究为ISAC在实际交通场景中的应用提供了可行方案，代码已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) combines sensing and communication to efficiently utilize wireless resources and is emerging as a key paradigm for next-generation wireless networks. By leveraging the wide bandwidth, high frequencies, and massive antenna arrays of 5G-Advanced and 6G systems, ISAC enables physical-layer sensing using Channel State Information (CSI). The 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) Release 19 identifies 32 potential ISAC use cases, with particular emphasis on detecting and tracking moving objects. In this work, we address the Sensing for Railway Intrusion Detection use case, where intruders, including wildlife, entering a railway track can pose serious collision risks. We generated 22,695 CSI matrices with corresponding ground truth using a 3D-rendered railway environment and the Sionna radio simulator. We developed a machine learning model combining a three-dimensional Convolutional Neural Network (3D CNN) and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) network to detect intruders in the track danger zone and estimate their real-time position relative to the train, velocity, and time to collision. On synthetic CSI data, the model achieves 99.57% intruder-detection accuracy on a balanced test set and a combined Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.4240 for position, velocity, and time-to-collision prediction. These results demonstrate the potential of CSI-based ISAC sensing with machine learning for reliable railway intrusion detection. The complete codebase for CSI generation, preprocessing, and model development is publicly available at https://github.com/EdgeIntelligenceLab/6g-isac-railway-intrusion-detection.

</details>


### [301] [Attention, Anomalies! Handling Attention Layers in Unsupervised Federated Outlier Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04753)
*Mihailo Ilić,Miloš Savić,Vladimir Kurbalija,Mirjana Ivanović,Giancarlo Fortino,Dušan Jakovetić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习场景下记忆增强自编码器（MemAE）聚合技术的缺失，提出了一种新型的引导式联邦聚合方案，在非独立同分布数据环境中显著提升了无监督异常检测的性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制在MemAE中已被证明能有效提升集中式学习下的无监督异常检测效果，但在联邦学习场景中，针对MemAE架构的专用聚合技术尚属空白。联邦场景下非IID数据分布和资源受限边缘节点的挑战亟待解决。

Method: 基于对MemAE架构特性的深入分析，设计了引导式联邦聚合策略：在联邦学习框架下，各边缘节点本地训练MemAE模型，通过定制化的聚合算法在服务器端融合模型参数，以应对非IID数据分布带来的模型漂移问题。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的聚合方案在非IID数据集上表现更鲁棒，尤其在多边缘节点、数据不平衡的无监督异常检测场景下优势明显。该方法甚至能提升极浅层自编码器的性能，使其适用于资源受限环境。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了MemAE在联邦学习中聚合技术的空白，所提出的引导式聚合方案有效解决了非IID数据下的模型融合难题，使MemAE能够在资源受限的联邦边缘场景中高效部署于无监督异常检测任务。

Abstract: Attention layers are the backbone of today's most powerful and impactful models. Models with multi-million and billion parameters rely on contextual knowledge provided by attention layers. However, their use goes well beyond just being the core component of large language models. One particularly interesting application is in Memory Augmented Autoencoders (MemAE), specifically for unsupervised representation learning in outlier detection tasks. It was shown that attention helps these models be more effective in centralized learning scenarios. Our work aims to address the lack of specialized aggregation techniques in Federated Learning (FL) when it comes to MemAE models. In this paper we analyze the intricacies of the architecture behind Memory Augmented Autoencoders, and propose novel, guided approaches to effectively aggregate these models in federated scenarios. We demonstrate our approach on non-IID datasets and show that these novel aggregation schemes are more robust when dealing with numerous edge nodes in environments with unbalanced datasets, specifically for unsupervised anomaly detection scenarios. This approach improves the performance of even very shallow autoencoders, allowing them to be used in resource constrained environments.

</details>


### [302] [IMFACT: Counterfactual Explanations for Time Series via Intrinsic Mode Function Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04777)
*Udo Schlegel,Julian Rakuschek,Thomas Seidl,Andreas Holzinger,Tobias Schreck,Javier Del Ser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IMFACT框架，在经验模态分解（EMD）空间中对时间序列分类器生成物理上合理的反事实解释。方法将信号分解为固有模态函数（IMF），并用最近异类近邻（NUN）的对应IMF逐步替换，直到分类器翻转类别。在UCR两个基准数据集上，基于方差的IMF选择策略配合三个NUN在可靠性和合理性指标上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振动等振荡信号中，类别判别信息承载于特定频带。直接在原始特征空间中扰动信号进行反事实分析容易破坏其时域结构，产生物理上不可行的结果。因此需要一种在保持信号物理合理性的前提下生成反事实解释的方法。

Method: 提出IMFACT框架：1）利用经验模态分解（EMD）将输入信号分解为若干固有模态函数（IMF）；2）选择目标信号的最近异类近邻（NUN），将其IMF作为替换源；3）按一定策略逐步用NUN的对应IMF替换原始信号的IMF，直至分类器预测翻转到目标类别。评估了六种IMF选择策略，并引入多NUN循环扩展以提升生成质量。

Result: 在两个UCR基准数据集（FaultDetectionA、FruitFlies）上评估：基于方差的IMF选择策略配合三个NUN在可靠性和合理性指标上优于两种主流基线方法；在三个NUN间循环的策略在两个数据集上均取得最佳邻近度（proximity）。

Conclusion: IMFACT框架通过在EMD分解空间中操作，能够生成物理上合理且可靠的时间序列反事实解释。基于方差的IMF选择与多NUN循环策略在多个评估维度上表现优异，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Oscillatory signals, such as vibration, carry class-discriminative information in specific frequency bands; perturbing them in raw feature space for counterfactual analysis easily destroys their temporal structure and produces physically implausible results. In this work, we introduce IMFACT (IMF-based counterfACTuals), a model-agnostic framework for generating plausible counterfactual explanations for time series classifiers that operates in the decomposition space of Empirical Mode Decomposition. An input signal is split into Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), and selected IMFs are progressively substituted with those of a Nearest Unlike Neighbour (NUN) until the classifier flips to the target class. We evaluate six IMF-selection strategies and a multi-NUN cycling extension on two UCR benchmarks (FaultDetectionA, FruitFlies). The variance-based strategy with three NUNs outperforms two prominent baseline techniques on reliability and plausibility metrics, while cycling across three NUNs yields the best proximity across both datasets.

</details>


### [303] [Continual-Learning Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Parameterized Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04778)
*Xujia Chen,Xinyue Hu,Letian Chen,Yi Liu,Wenhui Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CL-PINN 通过将不同参数下的PDE视为连续学习任务，结合贝叶斯优化主动参数选择、任务级动态损失加权和稀疏物理约束回放，在有限计算资源下高效求解参数化PDE族，解决了现有ParamPINN训练低效、精度不均和泛化差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ParamPINN存在三大缺陷：训练效率低、不同参数下求解精度不均衡、对采样参数过拟合导致未采样参数泛化能力差。这些问题源于参数域内任务分配不合理和知识保留机制缺失。

Method: 提出CL-PINN框架，核心组件包括：(1) 基于贝叶斯优化的主动参数选择，高效探索参数域；(2) 任务级动态损失加权，平衡各参数任务的训练；(3) 稀疏物理约束回放，通过重放少量先前任务的配置点抑制灾难性遗忘；(4) 可选的参数子网络，增强参数依赖关系的建模能力。整个框架无需观测数据，在有限主动任务容量约束下运行。

Result: 在1个连续函数和4个参数化PDE共五个基准上的多种子评估表明：贝叶斯选择相比网格贪心搜索显著减少目标损失查询次数；稀疏回放有效缓解早期任务遗忘；在预设资源协议下，CL-PINN在整体精度和参数间精度均衡性上均优于固定采样和网格贪心基线。

Conclusion: CL-PINN为跨物理参数的PDE解泛化提供了实用路径，有望支撑大规模工程参数研究中可复用的物理信息代理模型。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) incorporate governing equations into neural-network training and can approximate PDE solutions without requiring large observational datasets. Parameterized PINNs (ParamPINNs) further take physical parameters as inputs, allowing a single model to represent a family of PDE solutions over a parameter domain. Existing ParamPINNs, however, still face inefficient training, uneven accuracy across parameters, and overfitting to a limited set of sampled parameter tasks, which can impair generalization to unsampled parameters. To address these issues, we propose a continual-learning physics-informed neural network (CL-PINN), which treats PDE instances at different parameter values as related tasks and learns them sequentially. CL-PINN combines Bayesian-optimization-based active parameter selection, task-wise dynamic loss weighting, sparse physics-constrained replay, and an optional parameter subnetwork to improve task allocation and knowledge retention under bounded active-task capacity. It requires no observational data and is designed to solve parameterized PDEs over relatively broad parameter domains under limited computational resources. Multi-seed evaluations on five benchmarks, including one continuous function and four parameterized PDEs, show that Bayesian selection substantially reduces objective-loss queries relative to grid-greedy search, while sparse replay mitigates forgetting of earlier tasks. Under the prescribed within-case resource protocols, CL-PINN generally provides higher and more balanced solution accuracy than fixed-sampling and grid-greedy baselines. CL-PINN offers a practical route toward learning PDE solutions that generalize across physical parameters and has the potential to support reusable physics-informed surrogates for large-scale engineering parameter studies.

</details>


### [304] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04788)
*Yi Yang,Cong Qin,Xiaodan Liu,Chishui Chen,Qing Dong,Yan Zhang,Cao Liu,Zhao Yang,Lu Pan,Jiaye Lin,Yi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OCSD 提出了一种观测校准的自蒸馏方法，通过对比完整回放视图与观测消融视图来消除回放脚手架带来的混淆效应，从而获得更纯净的 token 级监督信号，在多个环境和模型规模上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OPSD 方法在利用特权回放视图生成 token 级稠密奖励时存在混淆问题：回放视图引入的分数变化既包含特权信息，也包含回放脚手架本身带来的偏移，导致无法准确归因。尤其当未来环境观测作为特权信息时，重构扩展脚手架会显著扰动 token 分数。

Method: 提出 OCSD，通过构造两个结构匹配的回放视图（Full 视图包含真实未来观测，Observation-Ablated 视图移除该观测），计算二者分数之差得到"观测残差"（observation residual），以此剥离回放脚手架共享的分数偏移。随后将该残差用于调节高不确定性步骤上的 token 级 GRPO 更新，同时保留轨迹级更新方向。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 Search-QA 三个环境上，跨 Qwen3 三种模型规模进行实验，OCSD 持续优于强基线方法。诊断分析进一步证实校准后的残差与局部环境反馈的对齐度更高。

Conclusion: OCSD 通过观测校准有效解决了 OPSD 中的脚手架混淆问题，为 token 级信用分配提供了更可靠的监督信号，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model agents are commonly trained through reinforcement learning with sparse trajectory-level rewards, which offer limited guidance on how strongly individual tokens should be updated. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) addresses this by re-scoring generated tokens under a privileged replay view to obtain dense, token-level supervision. However, we identify a confounding issue: the resulting support may reflect both the privileged information contained in the replay view and score shifts induced by the replay scaffold, making it difficult to attribute the support specifically to that information. This issue is especially pronounced when future environment observations serve as privileged information, since replaying them requires reconstructing an extended scaffold that itself perturbs token scores. To resolve this confounding, we propose Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation (OCSD), which contrasts two structurally matched replay views, Full and Observation-Ablated, differing only in whether the actual future observation is present, to derive an observation residual that discounts score changes shared by the replay scaffold. OCSD then applies this residual to modulate token-level GRPO updates at high-uncertainty steps, while preserving the trajectory-level update direction. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA across three Qwen3 model scales show that OCSD consistently outperforms strong baselines. Diagnostic analyses further confirm that the calibrated residual aligns better with local environment feedback. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yiy1x/OCSD.

</details>


### [305] [Above-ground Biomass Estimation with Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04792)
*Ghjulia Sialellia,Linus Scheibenreif,Jan Dirk Wegner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对地理空间基础模型（GFMs）在全球尺度地上生物量（AGB）估算中的表现进行了全面基准测试。研究发现，作为冻结编码器使用的GFMs显著落后于全监督SOTA模型，而预计算的嵌入产品（如AlphaEarth Foundations和TESSERA）表现优异，其中在AEF嵌入上训练的模型取得了最佳整体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球尺度上从卫星影像准确估算地上生物量（AGB）对碳储量监测至关重要，但GFMs在定量回归任务（如生物量估算）中的实用性尚未被充分探索，现有基准测试多集中于分类与分割任务。

Method: 使用AGBD数据集，在PANGAEA基准框架下评估11种作为冻结编码器的GFMs，同时评估两种预计算嵌入产品（AlphaEarth Foundations和TESSERA）。将这些模型与全监督SOTA模型进行对比，评估其地理和时间泛化能力，以及与ESA CCI生物量产品在独立参考数据上的一致性。

Result: 作为冻结编码器的GFMs显著落后于全监督SOTA模型；预计算嵌入产品效果显著：在AEF嵌入上训练的MLP超越了基于AGBD特征训练的SOTA模型；在AEF嵌入（可选增强选定原始特征）上训练的SOTA模型取得了最佳整体结果，且在时空泛化方面更优。

Conclusion: 预计算嵌入产品是GFMs用于全球AGB估算的有效途径，其性能可超越传统全监督方法。GFMs在定量回归任务中的价值取决于其交付方式——预计算嵌入优于冻结编码器直接使用。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) from satellite imagery is essential for the large-scale monitoring of carbon stocks, yet it remains a challenging regression task at global scale. Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising machine learning paradigm to derive general-purpose representations from Earth observation data, but their utility for quantitative regression tasks like biomass estimation remains largely unexplored, as most benchmarks emphasize classification and segmentation. Here, we present a comprehensive benchmark of GFMs for global-scale AGB estimation using the AGBD dataset, a machine learning-ready benchmark spanning diverse biomes and geographies. We distinguish two ways in which GFMs reach practitioners: (i) models distributed as weights to be run by the user, which we evaluate as frozen encoders within the PANGAEA benchmarking framework; and (ii) models distributed as ready-to-use, pre-computed embedding products, for which we evaluate AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) and TESSERA. We compare 11 GFMs available on PANGAEA and both embedding products against a fully supervised state-of-the-art (SOTA) model, assess their geographical and temporal generalization abilities, as well as agreement with the ESA CCI biomass product on independent reference data. Our results show that GFMs run as frozen encoders substantially underperform with respect to the supervised SOTA model, whereas pre-computed embedding products prove highly effective. An MLP trained on AEF embeddings outperforms the supervised SOTA model trained on AGBD features, and the same SOTA model trained on AEF embeddings (optionally augmented with selected raw features) achieves the best overall result, while also generalizing better across space and time.

</details>


### [306] [MGSB: Manifold Gated Signature Branch Pressure-Domain Baseline Architecture for Two-Phase Pipeline Flows Under Distributional Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04805)
*Issah Suleiman,Sormeh Serpoosh,Nadine Elkholy,Hicham Ferroudji,Mohammad Azizur Rahman,Matthew Hamilton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多相流管道泄漏检测模型在流动状态分布偏移下性能退化的问题，提出了一种流态感知架构MGSB，结合流态条件特征融合、TT-RoughPath编码器和Mean-Teacher一致性正则化，在OOD场景下显著提升了检测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多相流管道泄漏检测模型通常在训练数据分布内评估性能，忽略了由流动状态转变（如气泡流向段塞流过渡）引发的分布偏移所导致的模型失效。这种OOD场景下的性能退化在工业实际部署中十分常见但未被充分重视。

Method: 提出Manifold Gated Signature Bias（MGSB）架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 流态条件特征融合机制，将流动状态信息显式注入模型；(2) TT-RoughPath编码器，用于捕捉多变量时间序列的路径特征；(3) Mean-Teacher一致性正则化，在半监督框架下增强对分布偏移的鲁棒性。评估采用留一组交叉验证（leave-one-group-out），并通过马氏距离分析确认保留组属于真正的OOD数据。

Result: MGSB在分布内检测F1达到0.930，OOD场景下F1达到0.783，在严重特征损坏条件下大幅优于CNN-LSTM和全连接基线。消融实验表明，OOD鲁棒性的主要贡献来自所提出的架构本身，而非训练策略。

Conclusion: 显式流态感知建模是提升工业多相流管道泄漏检测在OOD场景下鲁棒性的有效途径，且该架构具有传感器无关的特性，具备良好的实际部署前景。

Abstract: Leak detection models for multiphase pipelines often degrade when deployed under flow regimes that differ from training. Existing evaluations typically assess performance under in-distribution operating conditions, masking failures caused by regime transitions such as bubble-to-slug flow. We propose the Manifold Gated Signature Bias (MGSB), a regime-aware architecture combining regime-conditioned feature fusion, a TT-RoughPath encoder, and Mean-Teacher consistency regularization to improve robustness under distribution shift. Under leave-one-group-out evaluation, MGSB achieves a detection F1 of 0.930 and an OOD F1 of 0.783, substantially outperforming CNN-LSTM and fully connected baselines under severe feature corruption. Ablations show the proposed architecture, not the training procedure, is the primary contributor to OOD robustness, while Mahalanobis-distance analysis confirms the held-out conditions are genuinely out-of-distribution. These results show that explicit regime-aware modelling is a practical path toward robust, sensor-agnostic leak detection in industrial multiphase pipelines.

</details>


### [307] [Robust Control under Stationary Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04832)
*Konrad J. Mueller,Amira Akkari,Ben Wood,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"平稳模糊性"（stationary ambiguity）概念，用于解决仿真训练的策略因逐渐推断出潜变量而丧失鲁棒性的问题。通过在仿真器中构建平稳的潜状态滤波过程，使模糊性不随时间系统性衰减，从而训练出对潜因子变化持续鲁棒的策略，并在金融对冲任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真优化策略在实际部署时，常因仿真器参数从有限数据估计而导致参数不确定性。若策略随时间逐步推断出潜变量值，模糊性消失，策略会过度专业化、丧失鲁棒性。在金融对冲等许多真实系统中，潜因子会发生漂移（如波动率机制变化），因此需要策略始终保持鲁棒，而非随时间衰减。

Method: 提出"平稳模糊性"原则：要求仿真器诱导出关于潜状态的平稳滤波过程，使模糊性随系统状态变化但不随时间系统性衰减。具体方法包括：(1) 构建满足平稳滤波条件的仿真器；(2) 在训练中对仿真器参数进行随机化；(3) 合理初始化仿真器与策略。该原则可作为仿真器设计的通用建模准则。

Result: 在对冲问题上，基于平稳模糊性训练的策赂在真实市场数据上表现出持续鲁棒性，相较于传统方法（模糊性随时间衰减）具有更稳定的长期表现，验证了该原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 平稳模糊性是设计仿真器的重要建模原则，能够使策略对潜因子变化保持持续鲁棒性。虽以对冲为实验场景，该方法同样适用于其他由外生随机过程驱动且潜结构会漂移的序贯控制问题。

Abstract: Control policies optimized in simulation can perform poorly in the real system when the parameters $x$ of the simulator are estimated from limited data but the resulting parameter uncertainty is not represented inside the simulation. A common way to incorporate such ambiguity is to simulate each trajectory of the system under a randomly drawn value for $x$. Since the policy cannot observe the drawn value, it must initially choose controls that perform well across many possible parameter values. However, if the policy progressively observes the system, it can often gradually infer the value of $x$, so that ambiguity vanishes. Over time, the policy then specializes to its estimate of $x$ and loses its robustness. This is undesirable in many real systems, where latent factors are expected to shift. In financial markets, for example, a policy hedging a derivative payoff should remain robust to changes in the volatility regime. To induce such continual robustness, we propose training policies in simulators where ambiguity varies with the system's state but does not systematically decay over time. We formalize this requirement as stationary ambiguity: the simulator should induce a stationary filter process over the latent state. We show how to construct such simulators and demonstrate, on hedging problems, that policies trained under stationary ambiguity preserve robustness to latent factors over time, leading to strong performance on real market data. As a modeling principle, stationary ambiguity informs many simulator design decisions: which models make realistic simulators, how their parameters should be randomized, and how simulator and policy should be initialized. While our experiments focus on hedging, stationary ambiguity may also be useful for other sequential control problems driven by exogenous stochastic processes with shifting latent structure.

</details>


### [308] [Training Crossroads for Recurrent Vision Transformers: Recurrence, Neural ODEs, and Deep Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04879)
*Grzegorz Gruszczynski,Pawel Olszowiec,Michal Byra,Grzegorz Stefanski,Alberto Presta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对单块循环视觉Transformer（bViT）进行了受控实证分析，系统考察了三种训练与推理机制下的性能表现：循环深度与独立参数化深度的权衡、ODE求解器阶次的作用、以及深度监督对训练外推鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT的参数随深度线性增长，而单块循环ViT通过共享参数消除了这一增长。然而，目前缺乏对不同训练和推理机制下循环ViT行为的系统性实证理解，包括：循环何时优于独立参数化深度、连续时间视角下ODE求解器的真实作用、以及循环模型在训练时长之外的鲁棒性代价。

Method: 固定bViT架构，在CIFAR-100上统一协议下进行受控实验：（1）在匹配FLOPs和匹配参数内存两种条件下比较循环与独立参数化深度；（2）将残差循环块通过ODE求解器训练，区分状态减去向量场  ż=Fθ(z)−z 与黑箱向量场，并分析求解器阶次的作用；（3）通过阶段式深度监督探索准确率-鲁棒性前沿。

Result: FLOPs受限时标准ViT更优，内存受限时循环ViT提供更好的准确率-参数权衡；ODE求解器阶次的作用是引入架构偏差而非提升数值精度，且收益不均匀；阶段式深度监督不提升标称准确率，但在远超训练时长的外推中保持优雅退化，而朴素循环则崩溃至接近随机性能。

Conclusion: 循环ViT在内存受限场景下具有优势，但FLOPs受限时不如标准ViT。连续时间建模中需正确区分状态减去向量场与黑箱向量场，高阶求解器本质上是架构偏差。深度监督为循环模型提供了关键的外推鲁棒性，尽管以不提升标称准确率为代价。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong image-recognition performance, but their parameter count grows linearly with depth when each block is independently parameterized. Single-block recurrent ViTs (bViT) remove this growth by repeatedly applying one shared block. Rather than proposing a new architecture, we fix a bViT and provide a controlled empirical characterization of three training and inference regimes under a common CIFAR-100 protocol, asking: (i)~when does recurrence beat independently parameterized depth---at matched FLOPs or at matched parameter memory? (ii)~when a residual recurrent block is trained through an ODE solver, does solver order act as numerical refinement or as an architectural bias? and (iii)~what does robustness beyond the training horizon cost in nominal accuracy? We find that standard ViTs remain preferable when FLOPs are the primary constraint, whereas recurrent ViTs offer a better accuracy--parameter trade-off under memory constraints. Consistent with the standard view of residual networks as Euler discretizations of ODEs, the continuous-time analogue of a residual recurrent block is the state-subtracted vector field $\dot{z}=F_θ(z)-z$; although known in principle, this distinction is easy to violate when the block is wrapped as a black-box vector field, and we qualify the cost at few accuracy points. Because the vector field is learned jointly with the solver, higher-order solvers act as a solver-induced architectural bias rather than a numerical-accuracy improvement, and their gains are not uniform. Finally, stage-wise deep supervision traces an accuracy--robustness frontier: it does not improve nominal accuracy, but degrades gracefully far beyond the training horizon, where naive recurrence collapses to near-random performance.

</details>


### [309] [Consistency-Driven Co-Evolution for Self-Supervised Cross-Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04926)
*Xuehang Guo,Pengyuan Li,Tom Hope,Tirthankar Ghosal,Manling Li,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoCoEvolve 提出了一种跨图表（chart）、表格（table）和代码（code）三种模态的一致性协同进化框架，通过定义显式的一对一对应关系，利用表示间的一致性作为优化信号，在训练和测试阶段均无需额外标注即可提升跨表示理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表、表格和代码之间的跨表示理解存在三个核心挑战：(1) 表示间的关系本质上是一对多的，导致映射模糊；(2) 监督信号模糊且标注成本高昂；(3) 现有方法缺乏既方向自适应又表示通用的原则性优化信号。为此，需要一种无需额外标注、能利用表示间一致性进行自我优化的方法。

Method: 提出 CoCoEvolve 框架：(1) 将跨表示映射从一对多问题转化为显式的一对一对应关系；(2) CoCoEvolve@Train 在训练时沿 chart→table→code→chart 循环进行协同进化优化；(3) CoCoEvolve@Test 在推理时应用相同的一致性目标进行测试时协同优化；(4) 设计了 CoCoEvolve@Eval 评测套件，覆盖全部六个方向的跨表示任务。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，CoCoEvolve 在训练时和测试时两种设置下均实现了跨表示理解性能的提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式一对一对应和一致性驱动的协同进化，CoCoEvolve 有效解决了图表-表格-代码跨模态理解中的一对多映射和监督难题，为多模态表示学习提供了一种无需额外标注的通用优化范式。

Abstract: As chart images, tabular data, and visualization code play increasingly important roles across diverse domains, cross-representation understanding across these modalities poses fundamental challenges for AI systems: the relationships across representations are inherently \textit{one-to-many}, supervision is ambiguous and costly, and model optimization lacks a principled signal that is both direction-adaptive and representation-generalizable beyond task-specific objectives. We introduce CoCoEvolve to improve consistency across chart, table, and code representations. Instead of treating cross-representation mapping as a one-to-many problem, we define explicit one-to-one correspondences and optimize models using agreement between representations, without additional annotations. During training, CoCoEvolve@Train performs co-evolution across the chart-table-code cycle, while CoCoEvolve@Test applies the same consistency objective at inference time for test-time co-optimization. We also present CoCoEvolve@Eval, an evaluation suite covering all six cross-representation tasks. Across four benchmarks, CoCoEvolve improves performance in both training-time and test-time settings. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/CoCoEvolve/.

</details>


### [310] [Optimal Training-Time Scaling in Gradual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04927)
*Zonghuan Xu,Krishna Harish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了渐进式适应(gradual adaptation)中，随着中间任务数量增加，每个任务的最优训练时间应如何变化。针对过参数化线性回归任务，理论分析表明最优单任务训练时间与任务数成反比，即 $s_N^\star = \Theta(N^{-1})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在渐进式适应（如持续学习、课程学习）中，模型需要依次在逐渐变化的任务上训练。一个核心实践问题是：当任务数量增加时，每个任务上的训练时间应如何调整才能最大化最终学习效果？此前缺乏对此问题的理论理解。

Method: 作者在过参数化线性回归框架下进行分析，假设任务平滑变化且共享一个零损失解。定义 $N$ 个任务、每个任务训练时间 $s_N$，分析当 $N s_N \to \tau$ 时最终学习进展的极限行为，并推导最优训练时间分配。

Result: 理论结果表明：极限学习进展在 $\tau$ 较小时为 $\Theta(\tau)$，在 $\tau$ 较大时为 $\Theta(\tau^{-1})$，呈现先增后减的非单调特性。由此导出最优单任务训练时间为 $s_N^\star = \Theta(N^{-1})$，即总训练时间 $N s_N^\star = \Theta(1)$ 为常数。在渐变旋转MNIST和Yearbook时间偏移数据集上的实验验证了路径划分越细、每任务所需训练时间越少的结论。

Conclusion: 渐进式适应中存在最优训练时间分配：任务数越多，每个任务的训练时间应反比例减少。过长或过短的训练都会损害最终性能，这一理论预测与实验一致。

Abstract: In gradual adaptation, how should the training time on each task change as the number of intermediate tasks increases? We study this question for overparameterized linear regression tasks that change smoothly and share a zero-loss solution. With $N$ tasks and training time $s_N$ on each, the final learning progress converges to a continuum curve when $Ns_N\toτ$. The limiting progress is $Θ(τ)$ for small $τ$ and $Θ(τ^{-1})$ for large $τ$, so both very short and very long training produce little progress. It follows that optimal per-task training times scale as $s_N^\star=Θ(N^{-1})$, equivalently $Ns_N^\star=Θ(1)$. Experiments on gradually rotated MNIST and a natural Yearbook time shift are consistent with less per-task training as the path is divided more finely.

</details>


### [311] [SVI-DAG: A Structured Variational Inference Approach to Bayesian Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04930)
*Shrenik Zinage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SVI-DAG 提出一种结构化变分推理方法，利用归一化流建模边间依赖关系，结合 Stein 变分梯度下降在无环空间中优化节点势函数，在贝叶斯因果发现中实现表达性强的多模态后验学习，在不确定性量化上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯因果发现方法存在两个关键局限：一是未能建模DAG中边之间的结构依赖关系，二是缺乏原则性的方式将领域先验知识作为归纳偏置引入搜索过程。此外，在庞大DAG空间上精确近似后验分布本身极具挑战。

Method: 提出 SVI-DAG 框架：(1) 采用归一化流（normalizing flows）建模有向边之间的依赖关系，支持对DAG后验的表达性和多模态学习；(2) 使用 Stein 变分梯度下降（SVGD）在无环性空间中更新节点势函数，以缓解ELBO优化中的模式追逐行为并促进模式覆盖；(3) 支持将先验信念作为归纳偏置融入推断过程。

Result: 在5种最先进的贝叶斯DAG学习方法对比中，SVI-DAG 在不确定性量化方面表现优越，同时在结构准确性上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SVI-DAG 通过结构化变分推断有效克服了现有贝叶斯因果发现方法在边依赖建模和先验知识融合方面的不足，实现了更优的不确定性量化与有竞争力的因果结构恢复能力。

Abstract: Bayesian causal discovery seeks to determine the posterior distribution of causal theories, which are interpreted as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that explain the observed data. The resulting posterior allows systematic reasoning regarding epistemic uncertainty within these theories. Nonetheless, finding such graphs is difficult due to identifiability problems and limited observational data. Furthermore, precisely approximating posterior over graphs is challenging given vast range of potential DAGs. Recent Bayesian approaches have addressed some of these challenges, yet they remain limited as they fail to encode dependencies between edges, and lack principled ways to incorporate domain knowledge as inductive biases during the search process. To overcome these limitations, we propose SVI-DAG, a structured variational inference approach to Bayesian causal discovery using observational data and prior beliefs that uses normalizing flows to model dependencies between edges, supporting expressive and multimodal posterior learning over DAGs. To mitigate mode seeking behaviour in evidence lower bound optimization and promote mode coverage, we use stein variational gradient descent to update the node potentials using a kernel in acyclicity space. We evaluate SVI-DAG against 5 state-of-the-art Bayesian DAG learning methods and demonstrate superior performance in uncertainty quantification while remaining competitive in terms of structural accuracy.

</details>


### [312] [CheMLFlow: An Open-Source Platform for Cheminformatics and Materials Informatics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04942)
*Brendan Smith,Susana Lopez-Moreno,Eric Dolores-Cuenca,Sangil Kim,Jose L. Mendoza-Cortes,Nijamudheen Abdulrahiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CheMLFlow 是一个开源平台，用于构建和执行端到端、高通量、代理式科学工作流，旨在解决科学机器学习中数据获取、模型训练、验证等环节的编排瓶颈，提供模块化组件、标准化产出和自动化友好的可复现流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研人员在开发科学机器学习时，常需将数据获取、整理、表示、建模、验证、筛选、解释和报告等多个环节组装成可复现的流水线，即使其核心贡献仅涉及其中某一阶段。这种端到端的编排工作构成显著的效率瓶颈，亟需一个标准化平台来降低开销并支持跨方法和数据集的基准测试。

Method: CheMLFlow 采用模块化工作流组件设计，提供即用型参考流水线、标准化产物和评估输出。平台具备可插拔的表示与模型、确定性数据划分、显式运行产物记录、批量执行和报告生成等功能，支持配置驱动的工作流和结构化输出，可作为编码代理的实用接口，辅助用户构建实验、检查结果和总结发现。

Result: 该平台在量子力学性质预测、物理化学性质预测和生物活性预测等基准测试中达到了文献水平的性能，并展示了在时间序列数据集上的应用案例，证明其能力超越分子化学领域的局限。

Conclusion: CheMLFlow 通过配置驱动、模块化、自动化的设计，有效降低了科学机器学习工作流的编排开销，提供了一个可扩展、可复现的平台，并有望在代理辅助实验的新趋势中发挥关键接口作用。

Abstract: CheMLFlow is an open-source platform for building and executing end-to-end, high-throughput, and agentic workflows for scientific and technological applications. CheMLFlow targets a common bottleneck in scientific machine learning development, where researchers often need to assemble data acquisition, curation, representation, model training, validation, screening, interpretation, and reporting into a reproducible pipeline, even when their primary research contribution concerns only one stage. CheMLFlow provides modular workflow components, ready-to-run reference pipelines, standardized artifacts, and evaluation outputs that reduce orchestration overhead and support benchmarking across methods and datasets. The platform is designed to be extensible, reproducible, and automation friendly, with pluggable representations and models, deterministic splits, explicit run artifacts, batch execution, and report generation. As scientific software increasingly moves toward agent assisted experimentation, CheMLFlow's configuration driven workflows and structured outputs also provide a practical interface for coding agents to help users construct experiments, inspect results, and summarize findings under human supervision. This article describes the system architecture, core workflows, and benchmarks that reach literature performance for quantum mechanical, physicochemical and bioactivity property prediction, and use cases involving time series datasets demonstrating applications beyond molecular chemistry datasets.

</details>


### [313] [SpecRoll: Fast-Slow Verifier-Feedback Adaptation for Speculative Reinforcement Learning Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04962)
*Nhat Minh Pham,Duy Tung Doan,Thi Duyen Ngo,Vinh Van Nguyen,Khac-Hoai Nam Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecRoll是一种用于强化学习后训练的高效推测性 rollout 引擎，通过双时间尺度自适应（快速 Reflex 模块与慢速参数更新）和并发感知稀疏树验证，在不改变目标分布的前提下将 GRPO 的生成速度提升 1.26–2.15 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练中自回归 rollout 是效率瓶颈；推测解码可加速生成，但目标策略持续演化导致静态提议者过时，而频繁更新 drafter 又带来巨大开销。

Method: 提出 SpecRoll：① 轻量级 future-token heads 生成并行提议；② Reflex 模块利用延迟验证器反馈进行有界的、轨迹局部隐藏状态修正（无需反向传播）；③ 慢路径仅在检测到持续性能退化时更新 head 参数；④ 并发感知稀疏树验证与精确目标验证，保持目标 rollout 分布和 GRPO 目标不变。

Result: 在 1.5B–14B 的 5 个模型和 3 个数学推理数据集上，相比 vanilla GRPO，生成加速 1.26–2.15 倍，端到端加速 1.21–2.04 倍；在全部 15 个匹配设置上均优于 FastGRPO，平均成对端到端增益 1.18 倍。消融实验证实快慢两条自适应路径互补。

Conclusion: SpecRoll 通过双时间尺度自适应与精确目标验证，在保持 RL 训练正确性的同时显著加速 rollout，为 RL 后训练的高效推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but autoregressive rollout generation remains a major efficiency bottleneck. Speculative decoding can accelerate generation, yet applying it during RL is difficult because the target policy continually evolves: static proposers become stale, while frequent drafter updates add substantial overhead. We introduce SpecRoll, a speculative rollout engine that preserves the target model's sampling distribution while adapting at two timescales. Lightweight future-token heads generate parallel proposals, while our proposed Reflex module uses delayed verifier feedback to perform bounded, trajectory-local hidden-state corrections without backpropagation. A complementary slow path updates the head parameters only when sustained degradation is detected. SpecRoll combines these mechanisms with concurrency-aware sparse-tree verification and exact target verification, leaving the target rollout distribution and GRPO objective unchanged. Across five models ranging from 1.5B to 14B and three mathematical reasoning datasets, SpecRoll achieves 1.26-2.15x generation speedup and 1.21-2.04x end-to-end speedup over vanilla GRPO. It also outperforms FastGRPO in both generation and end-to-end time across all 15 matched settings, with an average pairwise end-to-end gain of 1.18x. Controlled ablations show that the fast and slow adaptation paths provide complementary benefits. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecRoll-26062006.

</details>


### [314] [EvolveNet: Collaborative Harness Evolution for Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04968)
*Jun Nie,Yonggang Zhang,Qianshu Cai,Yiu-ming Cheung,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveNet提出了一种协作式LLM agent harness进化范式，通过将经验提取推向数据端，在本地进化后仅聚合程序改编（而非原始经验），解决了分散工作负载下harness集体改进的难题，在五个场景中均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent harness进化方法假设所有执行经验可集中到单一优化器进行串行进化，但真实agent生态系统中，用户、组织和环境产生的是孤立、不可汇聚的经验流。最值得学习的经验恰恰是无法集中的经验，因此需要一种新的协作进化范式。

Method: EvolveNet包含三个核心机制：(1) 将共享harness广播至各数据本地的agent部署，各自在本地工作负载上进化；(2) 仅将进化后的程序改编（adaptations）而非原始数据进行聚合，引入scope-typed、evidence-guided的程序聚合机制来解决独立修改程序无法像模型参数那样平均且可能冲突的问题；(3) 将更新后的共享harness重新分发，使所有参与agent继承彼此发现的操作经验。聚合边界从原始工作负载上移至学习到的改编，实现多路进化搜索并发进行、降低串行深度。

Result: 在text-to-SQL、数据科学编程、竞赛编程、软件工程和agentic工作流五个场景中，EvolveNet在所有场景均改进了共享harness。异构工作负载下增益最大，消融实验表明改进源于不同agent改编的组合（composition）而非简单选择（selection）。

Conclusion: EvolveNet通过将经验提取本地化、仅聚合程序改编的方式，实现了隐私保护与集体智能的兼顾，为去中心化agent生态系统中的harness持续进化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: The capabilities of an LLM agent depend not only on its model but on the harness: the executable program that constructs context, invokes tools, verifies results, and recovers from failure. Recent work shows that evolving the harness yields persistent improvements without updating model weights. Existing approaches, however, assume that all execution experience can be routed to a single optimizer, which evolves one harness along a sequential trajectory. Real agent ecosystems violate that assumption: users, organizations, and environments generate isolated streams of experience that cannot be pooled, so the experience most worth learning from is exactly the experience that cannot be directly centralized. We introduce EvolveNet, a paradigm of collaborative harness evolution that moves experience extraction to the data. A shared harness is broadcast to data-local agent deployments, each of which evolves it on its own workload. Only the resulting program adaptations are composed into an updated shared harness and redistributed, so that every participating agent inherits operational experience discovered by the others. By shifting the aggregation boundary from raw workloads to learned adaptations, EvolveNet keeps workloads local and allows multiple evolutionary searches to proceed concurrently with reduced serial depth. Because independently modified programs cannot be averaged like model parameters and may conflict when composed, EvolveNet introduces scope-typed, evidence-guided program aggregation. Across five settings spanning text-to-SQL, data-science coding, competitive programming, software engineering, and agentic workflows, EvolveNet improves the shared harness in all five, with the largest gains under heterogeneous workloads, and ablations attribute the improvement to composition of adaptations from different agents rather than to selecting among them.

</details>


### [315] [MultiPathFormer: Towards a Foundation Model for Multipath Wireless Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05076)
*Blessed Guda,Kayley Sze,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiPathFormer，一种基于多径传播的自回归基础模型，将每条收发链路表示为有序的连续值路径令牌序列，通过下一路径预测进行预训练。结合环境RAG机制和首径码本，在延迟和功率等路径统计估计上提升高达59%，在多个下游任务中全面超越现有信道基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线基础模型通常对信道张量在子载波、天线或时间维度上进行掩码重构预训练，忽略了无线传播的物理特性。作者认为多径传播是无线信道的本质物理结构，应将其作为预训练的基本对象，以学习更具可复用性的无线传播表征。

Method: 提出MultiPathFormer，将每条收发链路表示为有序的连续值路径令牌序列，采用自回归Transformer架构进行下一路径预测预训练。引入环境检索增强生成（Environmental RAG）机制和首径码本（first-path codebook），利用环境先验知识改善路径统计估计。预训练覆盖27个环境，支持通过场景微调迁移至新环境。

Result: 路径统计估计（延迟、功率）提升高达59%；在下游任务中全面超越SOTA信道基础模型：平均定位误差5.57 m、Top-3波束精度0.914、视距分类精度0.994、信道估计NMSE 0.561。预训练后可迁移至未见用户，微调后在新环境中优于从零训练。

Conclusion: 路径级预训练能够学习可复用的无线传播表征，MultiPathFormer在多项下游任务中均显著优于传统的信道级基础模型，验证了以多径传播物理特性为核心的预训练范式的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have enabled training of wireless foundation models, which aim to support tasks such as channel estimation, beam prediction, and localization based on wireless signals. Existing wireless foundation models typically pretrain on channel tensors using masked reconstruction over subcarriers, antennas, or time but ignore the physical characteristics of wireless propagation. In this work, we propose to instead use multipath propagation as the fundamental pretraining object. We present MultiPathFormer, an autoregressive foundation model that represents each transmitter-receiver link as an ordered sequence of continuous-valued path tokens and pretrains with next-path prediction. We introduce an Environmental RAG (retrieval-augmented generation) mechanism and a first-path codebook on top of the transformer backbone, leveraging environment knowledge to improve path statistics estimation like delay and power by up to 59%. MultiPathFormer pretrained on 27 environments transfers to unseen users and, after scenario-specific fine-tuning, outperforms training the corresponding models from scratch in new environments. Across downstream tasks, it outperforms SOTA channel-based foundation models, achieving 5.57 m mean localization error, 0.914 top-3 beam accuracy, 0.994 line-of-sight classification accuracy, and 0.561 channel estimation NMSE. These results show that path-level pretraining can learn reusable representations of wireless propagation.

</details>


### [316] [Optimizing What Policies Learn From: Recoverability-aware Rollout Intervention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05080)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAIL 提出一种基于可恢复性感知的干预学习框架，将 rollout 生成建模为在线上下文赌博机问题，通过 shadow-to-live 机制动态学习何时、何地以及如何干预，从而在有限 rollout 预算下产生更有信息量、更低冗余的学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于群体的免评论家强化学习方法在 LLM 后训练中，对每个任务和轨迹状态分配等量 rollout，忽略了不同 rollout 在学习信号价值上的差异。近期虽有自适应 rollout 生成的尝试，但存在两大局限：(1) 干预策略基于固定启发式，无法随策略训练演变而调整；(2) 仅决定生成多少 rollout，未显式控制干预的位置和方式。

Method: 提出 RAIL 框架，将干预选择建模为在线上下文赌博机问题。核心组件是一个可恢复性控制器（recoverability controller），通过 shadow-to-live 流程收集干预轨迹进行训练：先在影子策略上评估干预效果，再将学到的干预策略部署到实时训练中，使控制器能在底层策略持续演化时保持学习。该控制器根据每次干预产生的改进量来学习如何生成 rollout，同时决定干预的时机、位置和方式。

Result: 在多个实验设置下，RAIL 在有限 rollout 预算下一致提升性能，验证了其在有效性、适应性、表达性和效率四个维度的优势。

Conclusion: 基于可恢复性感知的干预为生成更具信息量、更低冗余的 rollout 提供了一种原则性方法，能为 LLM 后训练提供更强的学习信号，推动自适应 rollout 生成从固定启发式走向动态学习范式。

Abstract: Critic-free group-based reinforcement learning has become a scalable approach for post-training large language models. However, most existing methods allocate the same number of rollouts to every task and trajectory state, even though some rollouts provide much more useful learning signals than others. Recent work has started to treat rollout generation as an adaptive decision, but two important limitations remain. First, intervention strategies are often based on fixed heuristics and therefore cannot adjust as the policy changes during training. Second, these methods usually decide only how many rollouts to generate, without explicitly controlling where and how to intervene. To address these limitations, we propose Recoverability-Aware Intervention Learning (RAIL), a training-time framework that learns how to generate rollouts based on the improvement produced by each intervention. RAIL models intervention selection as an online contextual-bandit problem and trains a recoverability controller using intervention traces collected through a shadow-to-live procedure. This allows the controller to keep learning while the underlying policy evolves. We evaluate RAIL in terms of effectiveness, adaptivity, expressiveness, and efficiency. Across multiple settings, RAIL consistently improves performance under limited rollout budgets. These results show that recoverability-aware intervention provides a principled way to generate more informative and less redundant rollouts, leading to stronger learning signals during post-training.

</details>


### [317] [Learning When to Stop: Prefix-Optimal Dynamic Diffusion Policies for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05084)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POGP 通过学习去噪链上每一步的前缀价值函数，实现推理时自适应早停，在保持任务性能的同时将去噪迭代次数减少约2.7倍，且前缀训练作为辅助目标还能提升最终策略性能约3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略虽然强大，但其迭代去噪过程带来了显著的计算开销。现有方法缺乏一种既能根据每个动作的难度自适应调整去噪步数、又不牺牲任务性能的机制。

Method: 提出 POGP 框架：在去噪链的每个中间步骤，通过 Bellman 式递归学习一个「前缀价值函数」（prefix value function）。该函数有两个用途：(1) 作为辅助训练目标，鼓励中间去噪输出本身即成为高质量动作；(2) 在测试时提供停止准则，当额外去噪步骤不太可能产生有意义的改进时终止过程。

Result: 在四个 MuJoCo 环境中与12个基线对比：POGP 将所需去噪迭代次数减少约 2.7 倍，同时几乎保持全部任务性能；与最先进的动态扩散基线相比，前缀训练使最终任务性能提升约 3.5%。

Conclusion: 监督中间去噪步骤不仅对自适应早停有用，作为辅助训练目标还能改善所学策略的质量，为扩散策略的效率与性能双提升提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are a powerful policy class for continuous control, but their iterative denoising process creates a substantial computational bottleneck. Reducing this cost requires adapting the number of denoising steps to the difficulty of each action while preserving task performance. We introduce Prefix-Optimal Generative Policies (POGP), a framework that learns a prefix value function at every intermediate denoising step through a Bellman-style recursion over the denoising chain. The prefix value function serves two purposes: it provides an auxiliary training objective that encourages intermediate outputs to become high-quality actions, and it enables a test-time stopping rule that terminates denoising when additional steps are unlikely to produce meaningful improvement. Across four MuJoCo environments and comparisons with 12 baselines, POGP reduces the required number of denoising iterations by approximately 2.7-fold while retaining near-full task performance. Compared with state-of-the-art dynamic diffusion baselines, prefix training also improves final task performance by approximately 3.5%. These results indicate that supervising intermediate denoising steps is useful not only for adaptive early stopping, but also as an auxiliary objective that improves the learned policy.

</details>


### [318] [Capability-Gated Planning: Cost-to-Goal Discovery and the Limits of Myopic Experiment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05085)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，以短视信息增益最大化为目标的自动化科学发现系统存在结构性缺陷：当任务需要通过一系列"建设性行动"（如构建仪器、流程、模拟器）才能最终获得答案时，短视规划器无法为这些无即时信息回报的建设性行动赋予价值。作者将目标导向的发现建模为信念空间中的随机最短路径问题，证明了信息最大化规划器在有能力门槛（capability gating）场景下的无界近似比，并提出CG-Plan（能力感知的增量重规划器）来解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化科学发现系统普遍采用短视（myopic）评分策略（如单位成本期望信息增益）来选择实验，但作者发现这种策略存在结构性缺陷：某些"建设性"行动（如构建仪器、开发流程）本身不直接产生信息，其价值在于解锁后续可用的行动。当达到高置信度答案的最短路径需要一连串此类构建时，任何有限前瞻深度内的信息最大化规划器都无法为第一步构建赋予价值，因为它在地平线内不产生信息，会被任何产生正信息的测量行动所支配。

Method: 将目标导向的科学发现形式化为信念空间中的随机最短路径问题（stochastic shortest-path problem），其中建设性实验会改变下游行动图。提出"能力不可区分引理"（capability-indistinguishability lemma），证明在有限前瞻深度内，获取能力与支付空操作在观测上不可区分。在此基础上，提出CG-Plan——一种能力感知的增量重规划器，采用混合启发式函数 h = h_cap + h_exp，其中h_cap编码到达目标所需能力的成本，h_exp编码给定能力后的预期实验成本。在受控测试平台上验证了性能差距仅在有门槛（gating）时出现，且对任意固定地平线持续存在。

Result: （1）理论上证明：对任意前瞻深度d，存在实例使短视信息最大化规划器具有无界近似比，且在相关实例上永远无法到达目标。（2）将能力门槛（capability gating）确立为区别于子模性曲率（curvature）和自适应间隙（adaptivity gaps）的独立可达性困难维度。（3）CG-Plan在受控测试中有效弥合了性能差距，该差距仅在存在能力门槛时出现，且对任意固定地平线持续存在；当近误假设（near-miss hypotheses）来自数据一致性提议者时尤为显著。

Conclusion: 短视信息最大化在需要逐步构建能力的科学发现任务中存在根本性局限，能力门槛是与子模性曲率和信息序不同的独立困难来源。CG-Plan通过能力感知的启发式函数有效解决了这一问题，为自动化科学发现系统的规划提供了新方向。

Abstract: Systems that automate scientific discovery must repeatedly decide which experiment to run, which hypothesis to test, which tool to build, and when to stop. Many systems make these decisions by maximizing a myopic score such as expected information gain per unit cost or a learned plausibility score. We identify a structural limitation of this approach. Some actions are constructive: they acquire an epistemic capability (an instrument, assay, pipeline, simulator, or abstraction) whose value lies not in the information returned immediately but in the future actions it makes available. When the least-cost route to a confident answer requires a chain of such constructions, a planner that scores actions only by information obtainable within a bounded horizon cannot value the first construction: it yields no information within the horizon and is dominated by any measurement with positive information, however small. We formulate goal-directed discovery as a stochastic shortest-path problem in belief space in which constructive experiments change the downstream action graph, and prove that for every lookahead depth d there is an instance on which every myopic information-maximizing planner has an unbounded approximation ratio, and a related instance on which it never reaches the goal. The mechanism is a capability-indistinguishability lemma: within the horizon, acquiring a capability can be observationally indistinguishable from paying for a null action. This establishes capability gating as a reachability axis of difficulty distinct from curvature (submodularity) and information order (adaptivity gaps). We introduce CG-Plan, an incremental replanner with a capability-aware cost-to-go heuristic h = h_cap + h_exp. In a controlled testbed, the performance gap appears only under gating, persists for every fixed horizon, and arises when near-miss hypotheses come from a data-consistent proposer.

</details>


### [319] [MALT: Lightweight Curvature-Aware Muon via Diagonal Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05088)
*Tongle Wu,Huanyu Dong,Ying Sun,Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MALT 通过在 Muon 优化器基础上引入轻量级双侧对角预条件器来近似损失景观的曲率几何，从而降低曲率各向异性的敏感性；进一步提出 MALTER 变体以应对随机梯度噪声，并在 GPT-2 系列模型预训练中取得优于 Muon 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 通过 Newton-Schulz 迭代正交化动量矩阵来缓解梯度各向异性，但并未显式考虑损失景观的曲率几何，因此仍可能对曲率各向异性敏感。本文旨在填补这一空白，使优化器同时兼顾梯度各向异性和曲率各向异性。

Method: 提出 MALT（Muon Augmented by Lightweight Two-sided Preconditioning）：(1) 使用低内存和计算开销的双侧对角预条件器近似捕捉损失景观的曲率几何；(2) 对预条件后的动量执行 Newton-Schulz 迭代正交化，再映射回原空间定义更新方向；(3) 通过 norm grafting 控制更新幅度。进一步提出 MALTER，引入自适应步长重缩放以提升对随机梯度噪声的鲁棒性。文中还给出了随机非凸设定下的收敛性保证。

Result: 在 GPT-2 Small、Medium 和 Large 预训练实验中，MALT 和 MALTER 均优于 Muon，同时保持了几乎相同的内存占用和壁钟时间。

Conclusion: MALT/MALTER 通过轻量级双侧对角预条件成功弥补了 Muon 对曲率各向异性建模的不足，在保持计算效率的同时实现了更好的预训练性能，为大语言模型训练提供了一种更优的优化器选择。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a promising alternative to AdamW for language model pretraining by orthogonalizing momentum matrices using Newton-Schulz iterations. Although Muon mitigates gradient anisotropy, it does not explicitly account for the curvature geometry of the loss landscape and may therefore remain sensitive to curvature anisotropy. We bridge this gap by proposing MALT (Muon Augmented by Lightweight Two-sided Preconditioning), which uses lightweight diagonal preconditioners to reduce the sensitivity of Muon to curvature anisotropy. Specifically, MALT uses two-sided diagonal preconditioners with low memory and computational overhead to approximately capture the curvature geometry of the loss landscape. It orthogonalizes the preconditioned momentum using Newton-Schulz iterations and maps the result back to define the update direction, while norm grafting controls the update magnitude. To improve the robustness of MALT to stochastic gradient noise, we further propose MALTER (MALT with Adaptive stEpsize Rescaling). Convergence guarantees are provided for MALT in the stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on GPT-2 Small, Medium, and Large pretraining show that the proposed methods outperform Muon while maintaining nearly the same memory footprint and wall-clock time.

</details>


### [320] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal Atmospheric Data Assimilation with Latent Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05103)
*Dibyajyoti Chakraborty,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在视频流匹配的统一大气数据同化方法，利用ERA5再分析数据训练先验模型生成8天窗口内69个变量的连续轨迹，并结合后验采样同化真实观测数据（如无线电探空和地表观测），可灵活执行滤波、平滑等多种DA任务，直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报，性能与最先进的观测到预报模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化方法（如变分方法和集合卡尔曼滤波）通常依赖显式的误差协方差建模和复杂的循环同化流程。本文旨在提出一种根本不同的统一框架，利用生成式模型直接从数据中学习大气状态的时间演化先验，从而以更灵活的方式实现多种同化任务，并降低对显式物理模型和协方差估计的依赖。

Method: 核心方法分为两部分：(1) 先验训练：使用潜在视频流匹配（latent video flow-matching）在ERA5再分析数据上训练生成模型，该数据包含69个大气变量、覆盖8天时间窗口；模型能够采样生成时间上一致的连续轨迹。(2) 后验采样：在给定真实观测（如NOAA IGRA无线电探空数据和ISD地表数据）时，通过后验采样将观测信息注入生成过程。由于先验生成的是连续轨迹，信息可以在观测帧与未观测帧之间自然传播，因此仅需改变观测帧即可实现过滤（filtering）和平滑（smoothing）等不同DA任务。

Result: 该方法能够直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报，在观测到预报的任务上取得与当前最先进模型（state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models）具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 基于潜在视频流匹配的统一DA方法为大气数据同化提供了一种全新范式，它将先验学习与后验采样解耦，避免了传统方法中对显式误差协方差建模的依赖，并天然支持多种同化模式（滤波、平滑），展示了生成式模型在数值天气预报中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) uses Bayesian inference to update the state of a numerical forecast model with observed data. In this study, we propose a fundamentally different, unified approach to atmospheric data assimilation. We use latent video flow-matching to sample temporally consistent trajectories from a prior trained using ERA5 reanalysis (69 variables over an 8-day window). We also use posterior sampling to assimilate real observation sources, such as those from the NOAA Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive and the Integrated Surface Database. Because the prior generates a continuous trajectory, it naturally propagates information between observed and unobserved frames. Therefore, we can perform various DA tasks, such as filtering and smoothing, simply by changing the observed frames. Moreover, we generate full-state ensemble forecasts directly from sparse observations, achieving performance competitive with state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models.

</details>


### [321] [BnBERT-iPET: Sparse Few-Shot Language Modeling for Bengali via Lottery Ticket Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05104)
*Sajib Hossain,Md Kamrus Samad,Anan Ghosh,Labib Imam Chowdhury,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BnBERT-iPET，一种面向孟加拉语的稀疏少样本语言建模方法。通过彩票假说剪枝技术实现90%稀疏度，结合迭代模式利用训练（iPET），在仅保留BERT 10%边的情况下，该轻量模型在孟加拉语下游任务上与Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT、XLM-RoBERTa等大模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练模型（如BERT）在NLP任务中性能卓越，但训练成本高、耗时长、碳足迹大，对孟加拉语等资源受限语言形成技术壁垒。然而，深度网络中并非所有连接都同等重要，部分连接可被移除而不显著影响性能，这为模型压缩提供了可能。

Method: 提出BnBERT-iPET方法，核心包含两个关键技术：(1) 采用彩票假说（Lottery Ticket Hypothesis）剪枝技术，将初始BERT模型压缩至仅保留10%的边，实现90%稀疏度；(2) 采用迭代模式利用训练（iPET, iterative Pattern Exploiting Training）进行少样本学习，使稀疏模型能够从少量样本中有效学习。

Result: 实验表明，仅保留10%边的BnBERT-iPET稀疏模型，在孟加拉语标准基准数据集的下游任务上，性能与Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT、XLM-RoBERTa等更大规模的最先进语言模型不相上下（neck and neck）。

Conclusion: 通过剪枝与少样本学习结合，BnBERT-iPET证明了轻量稀疏模型可以在资源受限语言场景下有效替代大模型，显著降低内存占用和训练时间，同时保持有竞争力的性能，为低资源语言的NLP研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown impressive success in NLP tasks owing to their complex structure and huge number of edges. Achieving state-of-the-art performance in natural language processing with a large pre-trained model such as BERT is expensive and time-consuming, carries a large carbon footprint, and is difficult to realize on machines with minimal computational capability. This creates a barrier to training complex models for resource-constrained languages such as Bengali. However, in a complex neural model, not all edges are equally impactful, and the contributions of some of them can be neglected. Pruning promises to reduce the memory footprint of regular networks, shorten the training time of ever-growing networks, and increase inference efficiency without sacrificing comparable performance. In this work, we introduce BnBERT-iPET, a sparse few-shot language modeling approach for Bengali, and experimentally show that a lightweight few-shot-learned language model retaining only 10% of the edges of an initial model such as BERT can perform neck and neck with much larger models on challenging tasks for a resource-constrained language such as Bengali. By learning from few shots through iterative pattern exploiting training and achieving 90% sparsity with the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis pruning technique, our pruned BnBERT-iPET model proves to be a tough competitor to state-of-the-art language models such as Bangla Electra, Indic-BERT, and XLM-RoBERTa on downstream tasks over standard benchmark datasets of the Bengali language.

</details>


### [322] [Reward Structure Shapes the Interaction Between Episodic Exploration and Neural Memory in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05111)
*Jai Malegaonkar,Rohan Patil,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验交叉探索性奖励与神经记忆架构，揭示了探索与记忆在部分可观测强化学习中的互补关系：奖励诱导暴露，记忆将暴露转化为回报，且交互模式取决于奖励结构而非密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究孤立地评估探索奖励和记忆架构，缺乏对二者交互的理解；同时，稀疏奖励的标准概念混淆了时间信号密度与奖励实际监督的内容。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究探索奖励与记忆架构的交互机制。

Method: 在三种不同记忆获取方式的环境中，交叉使用回合式探索奖励与多种神经记忆架构进行受控实验；通过奖励操控验证交互模式取决于奖励结构而非密度；提出"观测锚定奖励机"（observation-anchored reward machines）形式化框架，将稀疏性分为结构稀疏性（自动机无需任务所需历史即可复现回报）和潜在稀疏性（单步奖励对局部探索行为定价错误）。

Result: 相同奖励信号产生三种交互模式：(1)当记忆内容需主动发现并无监督保留时，奖励放大架构容量差异；(2)当记忆内容为单一奖励监督线索时，奖励使各架构均达到共同上限；(3)当观测流纯粹按时间表给出时，奖励无效。密集奖励仅在直接监督所需潜在记忆时才能消除奖励效应；小的可规避探索惩罚会导致策略收敛至次优稳态，而探索奖励可解决该问题。

Conclusion: 探索与记忆是互补关系而非替代关系：探索奖励诱导对有用状态的暴露，而记忆是将暴露转化为回报的必要条件。所提出的稀疏性词汇为按任务所暴露的保留负担来组织不同的交互机制提供了有效框架。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning, agents face a dual bottleneck: they must explore to encounter rewarding states and retain that experience in memory to optimize their policies. Exploration bonuses and memory architectures are traditionally evaluated in isolation, leaving their interaction unmeasured, and standard notions of sparse reward conflate temporal signal density with what the reward actually supervises. We present a controlled study crossing episodic exploration bonuses with diverse neural memory architectures across three environments that vary how the content of memory is acquired. An identical bonus signal yields three distinct interaction patterns: it amplifies architectural capacity differences where memory content must be actively discovered and retained unsupervised; equalizes architectures to a shared ceiling where the content, once sought out, is a single reward-supervised cue; and is null where the observation stream is purely scheduled. Controlled reward manipulations verify that these patterns track reward structure rather than density: a dense reward neutralizes a bonus only if it directly supervises the required latent memory, and a small avoidable penalty on exploratory actions (leaving the optimum unchanged) induces policy convergence to suboptimal stationary states, which either bonus resolves. We then formalize reward sparsity with observation-anchored reward machines, separating structural sparsity (an automaton reproduces the return without the task-required history) from potential sparsity (the one-step reward misprices local exploratory actions); the resulting vocabulary organizes the three regimes by the retention burden each task exposes. Together, these results show exploration and memory are complements, not substitutes: a bonus induces exposure, and only memory converts exposure into return.

</details>


### [323] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种LLM引导的符号回归框架，将大语言模型嵌入迭代符号回归算法中，用于自动发现动力学模型。LLM在每轮迭代中执行两项任务：对最佳候选模型进行物理化学合理性评判，以及基于领域知识提出新的候选速率表达式。在四个涵盖多相催化和生物过程的案例研究中，该方法相比现有SR框架减少41.7%-79.3%的迭代次数，且LLM在超过半数引导运行中直接提出了正确的模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归虽能发现可解释的动力学模型，但通常在没有领域知识的情况下搜索，常探索物理化学上不合理的模型。大语言模型为注入领域专业知识提供了新途径，可有效约束搜索空间、加速模型发现。

Method: 提出LLM-guided SR框架：将LLM模块嵌入迭代符号回归算法。每轮迭代中，LLM执行（1）对SR生成的最佳候选模型进行定性物理化学评判；（2）基于SR模型和嵌入的化学知识，提出新的候选速率表达式。通过四个复杂度递增的in silico案例（涵盖多相催化与生物过程系统）进行评估，并进行消融实验。

Result: LLM引导框架相比当前最优SR框架减少41.7%-79.3%的迭代次数以识别真实模型；LLM在超过半数引导运行中直接提出正确模型结构；验证集上两种方法的R²均超过0.98；消融实验表明SR和LLM规模均对性能有贡献，且缩减规模的LLM仍能基本保持发现效率。

Conclusion: LLM可有效将领域知识注入科学模型发现中，大幅减少所需迭代和实验工作量，为全自动、领域感知的动力学建模流程铺平了道路。

Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

</details>


### [324] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了优化器的规范等变性（gauge equivariance）是梯度流低秩偏向能否被继承的关键。梯度下降、动量法、共享标量Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足该条件，而Adam、RMSProp等逐坐标方法则不满足。文章通过矩阵感知实验、谱调度分析及Transformer中注意力头的数值实验，证明基础选择（basis choice）决定了优化器所选的插值解，进而显著影响泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化模型 $W=UV^\top$ 上梯度下降会隐式偏向低秩解，但Adam从同样的小初始化出发却没有此偏向。该差异的根源尚未被系统阐明，亟需一个统一的理论框架来解释哪些优化器能继承梯度流的低秩归纳偏向，以及不能继承时会导致什么实际后果。

Method: 作者从损失的规范对称性（$(U,V)\mapsto(UQ, VQ)$）出发，定义优化器的规范等变性条件。给出结构定理：所有无记忆等变更新规则恰为Gram决定的左预条件子。建立迁移定理，将梯度流的路径性质迁移到共享标量流。在欠定矩阵感知任务上对九种更新规则排序，引入从逐坐标到共享标量预条件的一维参数族以隔离各向异性的因果作用。提出"谱调度"概念，并在Transformer中测量不同优化器下注意力头不变量的相对Frobenius距离。

Result: （1）规范等变性是低秩恢复的必要非充分条件。（2）一维参数族单调恢复低秩偏向，确证各向异性是Adam失去偏向的原因。（3）谱调度协调了Muon的矛盾报告：等速率更新可精确恢复低秩目标，但随着谱尾部增长失去优势。（4）在Transformer中，Adam第一步便将两个规范等价的初始化分离，最终注意力头不变量 $W_Q^\top W_K$ 的相对Frobenius距离达56%，无法通过任何逐头旋转弥合。（5）在两个高光谱数据集上，匹配训练损失时，梯度下降在最低采样密度下将held-out误差降低43-44%，且有效秩更低。

Conclusion: 基础选择并非调参细节，而是决定优化器选取何种插值解的关键决策。规范等变性是理解和控制隐式正则化的核心概念，对深度学习中的优化器选择、泛化性能及Transformer注意力头的表示学习具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [A Multi-Cohort Validation of Censoring-Aware Conformal Lower Predictive Bounds for Pathology Survival Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04025)
*Mingi Hong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文评估了drcosarc——一种基于事后共形预测的离散时间多实例学习生存分析包装器，在多个TCGA和CPTAC队列中检验其校准性能。结果表明，drcosarc在部分癌种中达到了接近0.90的目标覆盖水平，但整体表现呈现队列依赖性，且对患者层级单元、估计目标和删失假设敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片生存模型通常只提供风险排序，缺乏对个体事件时间的校准概率陈述。为此，需要一种能够为离散时间多实例学习生存模型提供有效预测区间的事后包装方法，并且需要在多中心真实数据和半合成数据上系统验证其校准性能。

Method: 提出drcosarc，一种固定截断的事后共形预测包装器，作用于基于冻结UNI2-h表征的离散时间多实例学习生存头。在5个TCGA队列上进行18种配置的内部扫描，在3个CPTAC队列上进行5种配置的外部评估。采用IPCW估计、中位预测下界、以及层级感知的患者集成估计量（drcosarc与朴素LPB差异的均值）进行评估。还进行了20次重复的低删失半合成实验，以及基于ABMIL的双队列敏感性分析（危害网格扩展至K=16）。

Result: 在α=0.1时，drcosarc的IPCW估计在KIRC、LUAD和STAD中最接近0.90。患者集成drcosarc-朴素区间在KIRC、KIRP、STAD、UCEC和CPTAC-CCRCC中排除了零，但在内部LUAD、CPTAC-LUAD、CPTAC-UCEC和内部LUSC扩展中包含零。半合成实验中经验覆盖率为0.9129 [0.9053, 0.9207]。探索性分析支持该数据生成过程中存在头部误差与删失的交互作用。增加危害网格至K=16提升了局部边际IPCW估计至0.87阈值以上，并产生正的配对LPB差异，但最差组的估计仍低于0.87。

Conclusion: drcosarc的校准性能整体呈现队列依赖性，其解释随患者层级单元、估计目标和删失假设的变化而改变。该方法在部分场景下可实现接近目标的覆盖水平，但仍需关注最差子组表现和数据生成过程中的删失交互效应。

Abstract: Whole-slide survival models commonly provide risk rankings without calibrated statements about individual event times. We evaluate fixed-cutoff drcosarc, a post-hoc conformal wrapper for discrete-time multiple-instance learning survival heads using frozen UNI2-h representations, in an internal 18-configuration sweep across five TCGA cohorts and an external five-configuration evaluation across three CPTAC cohorts. We distinguish configuration--fold--split summaries of the inverse-probability-of-censoring-weighted (IPCW) estimate and median lower predictive bound (LPB) from a hierarchy-aware patient-ensemble estimand of the mean drcosarc--naive LPB difference. At $α=0.1$, the drcosarc IPCW estimate was nearest 0.90 in KIRC, LUAD, and STAD. Patient-ensemble drcosarc--naive intervals excluded zero in KIRC, KIRP, STAD, UCEC, and CPTAC-CCRCC, but included zero in internal LUAD, CPTAC-LUAD, CPTAC-UCEC, and the internal LUSC extension. In a 20-replicate low-censoring semi-synthetic setting with known event times, drcosarc empirical coverage was 0.9129 [0.9053, 0.9207]. An exploratory analysis supported a head-error-by-censoring interaction within that data-generating process. In a two-cohort ABMIL sensitivity analysis, increasing the hazard grid to $K=16$ raised localized marginal IPCW estimates above the prespecified 0.87 threshold and yielded positive paired LPB differences, although worst-group estimates remained below 0.87. Overall, performance was cohort dependent, and its interpretation changed with the patient-level unit, estimand, and censoring assumptions.

</details>


### [326] [Regression-Based Proximal Reconciliation of Conflicting Trials with Unmeasured Effect Modifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04202)
*Daniel A Xu,Eric J Tchetgen Tchetgen,Enrique F Schisterman,Sean C Blackwell,Ellen C Caniglia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套因果推断框架，用于评估随机对照试验结果冲突是否可调和（reconcilable）。作者开发了基于代理变量的条件调和性检验方法，扩展了可移植性方法用于边际调和性评估，并引入了调和比例（reconciliation proportion）来量化边际调和程度。通过Meis和PROLONG两项关于17-α羟孕酮己酸酯预防复发性早产的冲突试验进行方法演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有相似方案的随机对照试验可能因效应修饰因子分布不同而产生冲突结果，但目前缺乏正式的统计框架来定义和评估冲突试验的可调和性，这阻碍了证据综合与监管决策。

Method: 作者构建了因果推断框架，在未测量效应修饰因子存在下，从加性和乘性尺度评估条件与边际可调和性。条件调和性检验：利用未测量效应修饰因子的代理变量，在参数化结构模型下进行回归检验。边际调和性评估：扩展现有可移植性（transportability）方法，建立等效性检验框架，并引入调和比例量化边际调和程度。

Result: 通过分析Meis和PROLONG两项冲突试验（17-α羟孕酮己酸酯预防复发性早产），发现以宫颈长度等未测量效应修饰因子的代理变量为依据，仅有有限证据支持两项试验在边际上可调和。

Conclusion: 近端调和性（proximal reconciliation）方法可帮助监管者、研究者和临床医生评估研究人群差异是否能够解释试验结果的冲突，为证据综合与决策提供新的分析工具。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials with similar protocols may yield conflicting findings when the distribution of relevant effect modifiers differs across study populations. Yet no formal statistical framework exists for defining and assessing whether conflicting trials are reconcilable, despite the importance of this question for evidence synthesis and regulatory decision making. To address this gap, we develop a causal inference framework for evaluating conditional and marginal reconcilability on additive and multiplicative scales in the presence of unmeasured effect modifiers. Within this framework, we use proxy variables for hypothesized unmeasured effect modifiers to develop regression-based tests of conditional reconcilability under parametric structural models. To assess marginal reconcilability, we extend existing transportability methods and develop an equivalence testing framework. We also introduce a reconciliation proportion to quantify the degree of marginal reconciliation. We illustrate these methods using the conflicting Meis and PROLONG trials of 17-alpha-hydroxyprogesterone caproate for preventing recurrent preterm birth. The analyses provided limited evidence that unmeasured effect modifiers such as cervical length, as captured by the selected proxies, were sufficient to marginally reconcile the trials. These findings demonstrate how proximal reconciliation methods may help regulators, researchers, and clinicians evaluate whether differences in study populations explain conflicting trial findings.

</details>


### [327] [When Is a Conformal Guarantee Fair? Auditing Silent Subgroup Under-Coverage in Alzheimer's Disease Longitudinal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04254)
*Lujia Zhong,Xinkai Wang,Shuo Huang,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套基于机制的框架，用于审计和修复共形预测在阿尔茨海默病生物标志物纵向预测中对高风险子群体的覆盖不足问题。研究发现，尽管总体边际覆盖达标，但高风险子群体（高遗传风险、高疾病严重度）在57/68个测试组合中存在显著覆盖不足，主要由"稀有性"和"重尾性"两种机制导致，并分别提出交叉共形池化、按子群体校准和覆盖安全的边际下限三种修复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病生物标志物的纵向预测日益影响临床决策，预测的可信度报告至关重要。共形预测虽能在可交换性假设下提供有限样本覆盖保证，但标准的总体边际覆盖保证可能掩盖临床重要子群体内部的严重覆盖不足，这对高风险患者尤其危险。

Method: 在ADNI和OASIS-3两个队列上，使用两种基础预测器，跨越遗传风险、人口统计和临床严重度共九个属性，系统审计了68种组合的子群体覆盖情况。识别出两种失败机制：(A) 稀有性——仅基于n个患者校准的群体条件预测带最多覆盖k/(n+1)；(B) 重尾性——总体预测带对重尾子群体过窄，且增加数据无法弥补差距。针对每种机制提出对应的共形修正：交叉共形池化应对稀有性、按子群体校准应对重尾性、以及两者并存时的覆盖安全边际下限。

Result: 在68个审计组合中，57个出现高风险子群体覆盖不足。覆盖不足不成比例地集中在高遗传风险和疾病严重度患者身上（平均赤字6.1个百分点，95% CI [3.3, 8.9]），而人口统计群体平均维持在目标水平（0.0 pp, CI [-1.9, 1.7]）。所提出的三种修正策略在两个队列和两种预测器上几乎为所有高风险子群体恢复了目标覆盖水平。

Conclusion: 共形预测在临床部署中不能仅依赖总体边际覆盖保证，必须审计子群体覆盖。稀有性和重尾性是导致覆盖失败的两大核心机制，对应的三种修正策略（交叉共形池化、按子群体校准、覆盖安全边际下限）可以有效修复问题，为共形预测在临床高风险场景中的可靠应用提供了机制驱动的解决方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal prediction of Alzheimer's disease biomarkers increasingly informs clinical decisions, and a forecast is only useful if it also reports how much to trust it. Conformal prediction supplies this by wrapping any forecaster in a prediction band with a finite-sample coverage guarantee under exchangeability. However, standard population-level conformal prediction guarantees only marginal coverage and may mask substantial under-coverage within clinically important subgroups. We introduce a general mechanism-driven framework for auditing and repairing such subgroup under-coverage. Across two cohorts (ADNI, OASIS-3), two base forecasters, and nine attributes spanning genetic risk, demographics, and clinical severity, we find that population-level bands under-cover high-risk subgroups in 57 of 68 audited combinations, despite achieving nominal marginal coverage. We trace these failures to two mechanisms: (A) \emph{rarity}, where a group-conditional band calibrated on only $n$ patients covers at most $k/(n+1)$; and (B) \emph{tail-heaviness}, where a population-wide band is too narrow for a heavy-tailed subgroup and additional data cannot close the gap. Under-coverage falls disproportionately on patients with high genetic risk and disease severity (6.1 pp mean deficit, 95\% CI [3.3, 8.9]), while demographic groups remain at the target level on average (0.0 pp, CI [$-1.9$, 1.7]). We pair each mechanism with a corresponding conformal correction: cross-conformal pooling for rarity, per-subgroup calibration for tail-heaviness, and a coverage-safe marginal floor when both arise. Together, these corrections restore target coverage for nearly every high-risk subgroup across both cohorts and forecasters.

</details>


### [328] [Bivariate Prior Specification for Bayesian Decision Making in Early Phase Clinical Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04335)
*Chengyuan Yang,Evan Kwiatkowwski*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在双变量Normal-Inverse-Wishart框架下的校准先验设定方法，用于贝叶斯Go/No-Go决策。核心发现是：先验精度κ是主导参数，驱动先验区分度；当κ=1时Go率差异仅0.07，κ=10时达0.56且假阳性率低于0.01。方法将先验设定简化为两项选择：先验中心与κ，并建议采用自适应的先验精度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多重主要终点（co-primary endpoints）的贝叶斯Go/No-Go决策中，需要在Normal-Inverse-Wishart框架下设定先验分布，但关于先验超参数如何影响试验决策的指导十分有限。研究者急需一种系统化的先验校准框架，以明确各超参数对操作特征的影响。

Method: 提出校准先验设定框架：将怀疑先验（skeptical prior）和热情先验（enthusiastic prior）分别校准，使其各自对临床相关决策区域赋予目标概率。证明了对任意先验精度κ>0，存在唯一的尺度参数λ₀可达成目标校准。通过模拟评估不同κ下的操作特征（Go率、假阳性率等），并应用于telitacicept狼疮III期试验。同时考察自由度ν₀和先验相关系数ρ₀的影响。

Result: 模拟结果表明：（1）κ是驱动先验区分度的主要参数；（2）κ=1时两种先验的Go率差异仅0.07，κ=10时达0.56，且假阳性率均低于0.01；（3）操作特征对自由度ν₀和先验相关系数ρ₀稳健，推荐默认ν₀=2；（4）狼疮应用中，κ=1时先验敏感性可忽略，κ=10时小样本下热情先验Go率为怀疑先验的三倍。

Conclusion: 该框架将先验设定简化为两个选择：先验中心和先验精度κ。κ被识别为主导参数，且试验前应谨慎选择κ，这为自适应先验精度方法提供了动机。推荐ν₀=2作为默认值，ρ₀影响有限。

Abstract: Bayesian Go/No-Go decisions with co-primary endpoints require specifying prior distributions under the Normal-Inverse-Wishart framework; however guidance on how prior hyperparameters influence trial decisions remains limited. We propose a calibrated prior specification framework for bivariate Go/No-Go decisions. Skeptical and enthusiastic priors are calibrated so that each assigns a target probability to a clinically relevant decision region. We prove that for any prior precision $κ> 0$, a unique scale parameter $λ_0$ achieves the target calibration. Operating characteristics are evaluated across different $κ$ via simulation and applied to a phase~3 telitacicept lupus trial.The simulation result indicates $κ$ is the primary driver of prior discrimination. At $κ= 1$, the go rate difference between priors was 0.07; at $κ= 10$ it reached 0.56, with false positive rates below 0.01. Operating characteristics were robust to the degrees of freedom parameter $ν_0$ and prior correlation $ρ_0$, supporting a default of $ν_0 = 2$. In the lupus application, prior sensitivity was negligible at $κ= 1$ but at $κ= 10$ the enthusiastic go rate was three times the skeptical rate at small sample sizes. The framework reduces prior specification to two choices: the prior center and the prior precision $κ$. The identification of $κ$ as the dominant parameter, together with the cautious choice of $κ$ before the trial, motivates adaptive approaches to prior precision.

</details>


### [329] [Best for which estimand? A known-truth benchmark of longitudinal-matching and target-trial-emulation methods for time-varying treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04414)
*M. Ehsan Karim*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文论证了在非可压缩生存机制下，纵向匹配和目标试验模拟方法并非同一真相的竞争估计量，而是回答不同因果问题的方法。文章系统比较了四种主流方法在不同目标因果估计量上的相对效率、方差估计和模型敏感性，揭示了"幻影偏倚"、秩反转等关键现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述文献缺乏对纵向匹配和目标试验模拟方法在相对效率、方差估计和模型敏感性方面的直接比较。更重要的是，将这些方法放在同一个"真实风险比"基准下进行评分，实际上是在制造偏倚，因为它们本质上估计的是不同的因果估计量（边际、条件、处理组平均处理效应、意向治疗与符合方案等）。

Method: 作者在一个已知真相的连续时间、非可压缩Cox机制上，比较了四种主流方法家族：序贯Cox回归、序贯分层、风险集匹配和IPTW边际结构模型。通过该机制清晰量化了：(1) 共享边际真相所制造的幻影偏倚；(2) 推荐方法随目标估计量变化而发生的秩反转；(3) 跨家族的方差估计表现（聚类稳健 sandwich 估计量与 bootstrap 子研究）；(4) 遗漏混杂因素和意向治疗/符合方案效应差异带来的模型敏感性。此外还在第二个机制上进行了复现验证。

Result: （1）幻影偏倚：匹配估计量为0.32-0.33对数累积风险比单位，条件方法为0.15，天真的时变Cox为0.76；（2）秩反转：推荐方法随目标估计量不同而翻转，低方差但偏离目标的估计量在均方误差上可能胜出；（3）聚类稳健sandwich估计量在试验堆叠估计器中名义覆盖率接近0.90，但在匹配估计器中仅为0.77-0.82，经n=500的bootstrap校正后提升至0.95-0.96；（4）遗漏混杂导致0.45-0.50对数风险比偏倚且覆盖率不足；意向治疗和符合方案效应随治疗转换增加而分化。心脏移植分析佐证了上述发现。第二个机制上四项发现中三项得到复现，秩反转减弱，模型敏感性表现出校准依赖性。

Conclusion: 纵向匹配和目标试验模拟方法并非竞争关系，而是回答不同因果问题的互补工具。将它们置于单一"真实风险比"基准下比较会制造幻影偏倚，且这种偏倚在方法间差异显著（可达0.32-0.33对数单位）。研究者的方法选择应明确基于目标因果估计量，而非盲目追求单一指标的优劣。此外，方差估计策略和模型敏感性在不同方法家族间表现迥异，需在应用时谨慎对待。

Abstract: On a non-collapsible survival mechanism, longitudinal-matching and target-trial-emulation methods are not competing estimators of one truth but answers to different causal questions, so a benchmark that scores them against a single "true hazard ratio" fabricates bias. We provide the direct comparison of relative efficiency, variance estimation, and model sensitivity that reviews find lacking. On a deliberately non-collapsible continuous-time Cox mechanism with known truth, the dominant families (sequential Cox, sequential stratification, risk-set matching, and inverse-probability-of-treatment-weighted (IPTW) marginal structural models) target numerically distinct causal estimands (marginal, conditional, two average-treatment-effect-on-the-treated, and intention-to-treat versus per-protocol). First, we quantify the phantom bias a shared marginal truth fabricates: 0.32-0.33 log-cumulative-hazard-ratio units for the matching estimators and 0.15 for the conditional method; the associational naive time-dependent Cox sits 0.76 away, a total discrepancy compounding the estimand gap with confounding. Second, a rank reversal: the recommended method flips with the target estimand, and a low-variance off-target estimator can still win on mean-squared error. Third, a cross-family variance result: the cluster-robust sandwich is closer to nominal for the trial-stacking estimator (0.90) but under-covers the matching estimators (0.77-0.82), which a prespecified n=500 bootstrap sub-study brings to 0.95-0.96. Fourth, model sensitivity: omitting a confounder induces 0.45-0.50 log-hazard-ratio bias and undercoverage, and intention-to-treat and per-protocol effects diverge as switching increases; a heart-transplant analysis illustrates these. On a second mechanism three of four findings replicate, the rank reversal attenuating and model sensitivity proving calibration-dependent.

</details>


### [330] [Semiparametric robust mixture of experts based on nonparametric maximum likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04561)
*Sangkon Oh,Victor H. Lachos,Byungtae Seo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种半参数混合专家模型，通过非参数高斯尺度混合表示各专家误差分布，以非参数最大似然估计，从而放松参数假设、提升对离群值和厚尾的鲁棒性，同时在正确指定高斯设定下保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的专家误差分布多依赖参数假设（通常为高斯分布），当假设错误时，对离群值和厚尾数据敏感，导致估计效率低下。需要在保持MoE框架可解释性的同时，放松参数假设以提高鲁棒性。

Method: 提出半参数MoE模型，将每个专家的误差分布表示为非参数高斯尺度混合，并通过非参数最大似然（NPMLE）进行估计。该建模策略在高斯尺度混合族内放松参数约束，但保留MoE的协变量依赖门控结构。

Result: 模型能够适应复杂的误差结构，在存在污染和厚尾情况下显著提升鲁棒性，同时在正确指定的高斯设定下表现与参数化方法相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的半参数MoE为传统参数化MoE提供了一个实用且有理论保障的替代方案，兼具灵活性与稳健性。

Abstract: The mixture of experts (MoE) model provides a flexible approach for modeling heterogeneous regression relationships by allowing covariate-dependent mixing through a gating network, but most existing MoE models rely on parametric assumptions for expert error distributions, typically Gaussian, which can lead to inefficiency and sensitivity to outliers or heavy-tailed behavior when misspecified. We propose a semiparametric MoE model in which each expert error distribution is represented as a nonparametric Gaussian scale mixture estimated via nonparametric maximum likelihood, relaxing parametric assumptions within the Gaussian scale-mixture class while preserving the interpretability and structure of the MoE framework. The resulting model adapts to complex error structures, improves robustness under contamination and heavy tails, and remains competitive under well-specified Gaussian settings, providing a practical and theoretically grounded alternative to parametric MoE formulations.

</details>


### [331] [Constructing Large Orthogonal Minimally Aliased Response Surface Designs Through Enumeration and Combination of Weighing Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04814)
*Jade Lejeune Herman,Peter Goos*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构建大型正交最小混叠响应曲面（OMARS）设计的算法框架，通过枚举和组合加权设计（每列具有固定数量非零项的三水平矩阵）来解决高维实验中计算效率与设计质量之间的矛盾。该方法实现了最多24次试验的完全枚举，并通过部分枚举和组合策略将目录扩展至更大规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量实验和自动化技术的发展使得实验规模和复杂度急剧增长，现有方法在构建大型实验设计时面临可扩展性不足的问题，难以同时保持正交性和最小化因子间混叠。因此亟需一种能够高效生成大规模、高质量实验设计的计算框架。

Method: 作者提出通过枚举和组合"加权设计"（weighing designs）来构建OMARS设计。加权设计是具有正交列且每列含固定数量非零项的三水平矩阵。具体方法包括：(1) 对最多24次试验的设计进行完全枚举，覆盖每个因子含2或3个零水平的多种因子数和权重组合；(2) 开发经过验证的部分枚举程序；(3) 提出组合方法，将基础规模设计的枚举结果扩展为任意为基础规模整数倍的大规模OMARS设计。

Result: 完全枚举覆盖了最多24次试验的设计空间；部分枚举和组合方法使得设计目录可扩展至远超此规模的实验。组合方法能够为任意基础规模整数倍的试验次数构建OMARS设计，生成的大型设计目录适用于高维筛选和响应曲面建模。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成大规模高质量OMARS设计目录提供了完整的方法论，所生成的设计在保持正交性和最小混叠方面表现优异，非常适合复杂工业和科学实验中的高维筛选与响应曲面建模应用场景。

Abstract: Advances in automation and high-throughput experimentation have enabled larger and more complex studies involving many factors and tests, creating a growing demand for computationally effective design construction methods. Efficient experimental design remains a key challenge in this context, creating a need for frameworks that can generate large experiments while preserving orthogonality and minimal aliasing. Unlike existing approaches which struggle with scalability, this work introduces an algorithmic framework for constructing large Orthogonal Minimally Aliased Response Surface (OMARS) designs by enumerating and combining weighing designs, three-level matrices with orthogonal columns and a fixed number of non-zero entries per column. Complete enumerations of weighing designs are achieved for designs with up to 24 tests, covering multiple numbers of factors and weights corresponding to two or three zeros per factor. In addition, a validated partial enumeration procedure and a combination method extend the catalog to substantially larger designs. The combination method enables the construction of OMARS designs for any test size that is a multiple of selected base sizes. This paper thus provides the methodology for generating large catalogs of high-quality OMARS designs, well-suited for high-dimensional screening and response-surface modelling in complex industrial and scientific experiments.

</details>


### [332] [Nonparametric Goodness-of-fit Testing under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04860)
*Zhen Hou,Dong Xia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了协变量偏移下的非参数拟合优度检验方法，通过截断重要性加权核岭回归与乘数自举相结合，在目标-源密度比仅满足有界矩或次指数尾条件下，构建了具有非渐近有效性的置信集，并给出了覆盖概率的显式误差率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协变量偏移场景下，标注数据来自源分布，而拟合优度评估需针对目标分布。现有方法通常要求密度比有界或具有轻尾特性，当密度比呈现重尾时，重要性加权估计器不稳定且自举校准失效。因此需要一种在弱条件下仍能有效工作的非参数检验方法。
