Method: 采用截断重要性加权核岭回归（Truncated Importance-Weighting Kernel Ridge Regression）估计回归函数，结合乘数自举（Multiplier Bootstrap）进行校准以构建置信集。截断机制同时稳定了回归估计和自举过程。理论分析在算子兼容条件下证明非渐近有效性与锐度，并在密度比条件及核积分算子谱衰减条件下导出覆盖概率的显式误差界。

Result: 在仅假设目标-源密度比满足有界矩条件或次指数尾条件（无需有界或轻尾）下，证明了置信集的非渐近有效性和锐度，建立了覆盖概率的显式误差率，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 截断策略有效扩展了协变量偏移下非参数拟合优度检验的适用范围，使其能处理密度比重尾情形。该方法在理论和实践上均表现出良好性能，为协变量偏移下的统计推断提供了更鲁棒的工具。

Abstract: This paper develops procedures for nonparametric goodness-of-fit testing under covariate shift, where labelled data are drawn from a source population but goodness-of-fit is evaluated for a target population. The distribution mismatch is quantified by either a bounded moment condition or a sub-exponential tail condition on the target-to-source density ratio. Our method combines truncated importance-weighting kernel ridge regression with a multiplier bootstrap to construct confidence sets for the regression function. The truncation stabilizes the importance- weighting kernel ridge regression as well as the bootstrap calibration, making our approach applicable even when the density ratio has heavy tails. We prove nonasymptotic validity and sharpness of the resulting confidence sets under suitable operator compatibility conditions, and establish explicit error rates for coverage probability under specific conditions on the target- to-source density ratio and on the spectral decay of the kernel integral operator. Numerical experiments corroborate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [333] [Inverse probability weighting for auxiliary variable dependent sampling in observational studies of Long COVID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04918)
*Andrea S. Foulkes,Tanayott Thaweethai,Daniel O. Scharfstein,Weixing Huang,Harrison T. Reeder*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文讨论了基于辅助变量选择性抽样的两阶段抽样设计在观察性研究中的分析挑战，以RECOVER COVID研究为背景，阐述了忽视抽样机制导致的偏倚问题及相应的分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由RECOVER成人及儿科观察性队列研究驱动，旨在解决电子健康档案数据中普遍存在的基于辅助变量选择性抽样（两阶段抽样设计）被忽视的问题——忽略这一抽样机制会导致有偏估计和错误的科学结论。

Method: 描述了两阶段抽样设计（auxiliary variable dependent sampling）下数据分析中常见的陷阱，并提出了一种严格的统计分析方法来正确解释这种抽样机制。

Result: （摘要未报告具体数值结果，主要聚焦方法论阐述）——指出严格的分析方法在观察性研究中仍未被充分利用。

Conclusion: 在基于辅助变量依赖抽样的观察性研究中，研究者必须正视并采用适当的分析方法来纠正选择性抽样带来的偏倚，否则可能导致错误的科学推断。

Abstract: Selective testing based on values of auxiliary variables is an increasingly popular design strategy in observational studies and is ubiquitous in electronic health record data. Ignoring this underlying sampling mechanism can lead to biased estimation and erroneous scientific conclusions. Yet, rigorous analytic methods for accounting for two-phase sampling designs in observational settings remain under-utilized. Motivated by the Researching COVID to Enhance Recovery (RECOVER) Adult and Pediatric observational cohort studies, we describe common pitfalls and an approach for analysis of data collected via auxiliary variable dependent sampling.

</details>


### [334] [Parameter identification for predator-prey system with sparse data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04959)
*Eduard Campillo-Funollet,James Van Yperen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自然梯度上升（Natural Gradient Ascent）的计算框架，用于在数据稀疏、含噪声条件下对生态动力学系统（以Lotka-Volterra捕食者-猎物模型为例）进行参数辨识。该方法利用无量纲化处理缩放因子作为冗余参数以降维，并采用自适应求解器在两个独立二阶方程之间切换以避免数值不稳定，相比标准梯度上升和BFGS方法收敛更快、更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态动力学系统的参数辨识面临三个核心挑战：(1) 生态数据通常稀疏且含观测噪声，导致似然函数不规则，标准优化方法难以收敛；(2) ODE求解器在参数空间某些区域会变得刚性或数值不稳定，导致运行时间过长或运行时错误；(3) 传统优化方法对初始猜测敏感，需要准确的初值才能保证收敛。因此需要一种更稳健、能应对这些数值困难的参数估计框架。

Method: 作者提出了一个综合计算框架：(1) 采用**自然梯度上升（Natural Gradient Ascent）**作为优化算法，利用Fisher信息矩阵对梯度进行缩放，使优化路径在参数空间中更具几何合理性；(2) 对Lotka-Volterra常微分方程进行**无量纲化**，将缩放因子作为冗余参数（nuisance parameters），从而降低优化问题的维度；(3) 设计**自适应求解器**，当某一分量出现数值问题时，在两个独立的二阶方程（分别从模型的 prey 和 predator 分量导出）之间切换，防止求解器步长过小而停滞。

Result: 在经典Lotka-Volterra捕食者-猎物模型上的应用表明，所提出的自然梯度上升框架相较于标准梯度上升和BFGS方法，能用更少的迭代次数实现收敛，且具有更好的数值稳定性。自适应求解器策略有效避免了ODE求解中的刚性问题和不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据有限的生态参数估计提供了一种可靠方法。只要动力学系统的不同分量不会同时出现数值问题，该方法可推广至其他动力系统。核心贡献在于将自然梯度上升、无量纲化降维和自适应求解器切换三者结合，系统性地解决了稀疏生态数据下的参数辨识难题。

Abstract: Parameter identification from observations of dynamical systems is a fundamental problem in population biology. Mechanistic models of ecological systems rely on optimization methods that require accurate initial guesses to guarantee convergence. In ecological applications, datasets contain observation noise and are collected at sparse time points. This sparsity creates irregular likelihoods that cause standard optimization methods to struggle, while the ordinary differential equation solvers can become stiff or unstable in certain regions of the parameter space. These instabilities cause long running times or runtime errors. Here we present a computational framework for parameter identification that addresses these numerical instabilities by employing Natural Gradient Ascent, and we apply it to the classical Lotka-Volterra predator-prey model. We exploit the non-dimensionalization of the ordinary differential equations to treat scaling factors as nuisance parameters, reducing the dimensionality of the optimization problem. To prevent the solver step from becoming small, we implement an adaptive solver that switches between two independent second-order equations derived from the two components of the model. This approach allows Natural Gradient Ascent to converge in fewer iterations and with more stability than standard gradient ascent or BFGS methods. This framework provides a reliable method for parameter estimation in ecology when data is limited. The method can be generalized to other dynamical systems as long as the different components of the system do not become numerically problematic at the same time.

</details>


### [335] [Nonparametric Estimation under General Nonlinear ODE Constraints: A Comparison with Parametric ODE-Fitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05081)
*Chunlei Ge,W. John Braun*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种微分方程约束的局部多项式回归框架（DE-constrained LPR），用于处理一般形式的一阶ODE约束 g'(x)=F(x,g(x))，其中F为Lipschitz连续函数。该方法通过逐次符号微分求解Taylor系数，采用非线性最小二乘估计，仅需单一局部参数。推导了渐近条件偏差与方差，并提出利用ODE结构的AIMSE最优带宽。模拟研究表明，该方法一致优于经典局部线性回归，并与参数级联法（PCODE）具有竞争力，尤其在曲线陡峭时更准确、更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理、生物和流行病学过程由状态变量非线性的ODE支配。现有DE约束的局部多项式回归工作仅限于指数和线性ODE结构，无法处理一般形式的非线性ODE约束。此外，当结构参数难以可靠识别时，传统参数化ODE拟合方法（如PCODE）表现不稳定，亟需一种实用的非参数替代方法。

Method: 将DE约束以罚函数形式嵌入局部多项式回归，针对一阶ODE g'(x)=F(x,g(x))构建DE1-k估计器。由于F对g非线性，Taylor系数无法闭式写出，通过F的逐次符号微分获得，并使用非线性最小二乘求解，每个评估点仅需一个局部参数。推导了估计器的渐近条件偏差与方差，并提出AIMSE最优带宽选择方法，该方法利用ODE结构避免了高阶导数的直接估计。

Result: 基于Logistic增长的模拟研究中，DE约束估计器一致优于局部线性回归；与PCODE方法相比具有竞争力，尽管DE方法不估计ODE的任何结构参数。灵敏度分析表明，随曲线变陡、PCODE参数估计稳定性下降时，DE约束估计的准确性和稳健性进一步提升。

Conclusion: DE约束LPR为参数化ODE拟合方法提供了一种实用的非参数替代方案，特别适用于结构参数难以可靠识别的场景，具有良好的准确性和稳健性。

Abstract: Many physical, biological, and epidemiological processes are governed by ordinary differential equations (ODEs) that are nonlinear in the state variable, including logistic population growth, chemical reaction kinetics, and epidemiological compartment models. We develop a differential equation-constrained local polynomial regression (DE-constrained LPR) framework for the general first-order ODE constraint g'(x) = F(x, g(x)), where F may be any Lipschitz continuous function, extending prior work restricted to exponential and linear ODE structures. Because F is generally nonlinear in g, the Taylor coefficients of the DE1-k estimator cannot be written in closed form; instead they are obtained by successive symbolic differentiation of F, and the estimator is computed by nonlinear least squares, requiring only a single local parameter at each evaluation point regardless of polynomial degree k. We derive the asymptotic conditional bias and variance of the DE1-k estimator, propose an AIMSE-optimal bandwidth that exploits the ODE structure to avoid direct estimation of high-order derivatives, and evaluate the method in a simulation study based on logistic growth, benchmarking against the parameter cascading method of Ramsay et al. (2007) (PCODE) and classical local linear regression. The DE-constrained estimator consistently outperforms local linear regression and is competitive with PCODE even though it estimates no structural parameter of the ODE; a sensitivity analysis across growth rates shows DE-constrained estimation becomes more accurate and more robust than PCODE as the curve steepens and PCODE's parameter estimation grows less stable. These results position DE-constrained LPR as a practical nonparametric alternative to parametric ODE-fitting methods when structural parameters are difficult to identify reliably.

</details>


### [336] [Micro-randomized Trials with Categorical Treatments and Binary Proximal Outcome: Causal Effect Estimation and Sample Size Calculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05135)
*Jeremy Lin,Tianchen Qian*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对具有分类处理变量和二元近端结局的微随机化试验（MRT），定义了因果偏移效应（causal excursion effect），提出了EMEE-catA估计量，并推导了用于比较不同分类处理水平的样本量公式，通过模拟研究验证了其性能，并在Drink Less MRT数据上进行了方法演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康（mHealth）干预中，处理常为多水平分类变量（如不同消息内容或推送策略），且长期结局常为二元变量（如是否打开应用、是否完成目标行为）。现有MRT方法对此类场景的样本量规划和效应估计支持不足，亟需专门的方法学框架。

Method: 定义因果偏移效应（causal excursion effect）作为感兴趣估计量；提出EMEE-catA估计量（针对分类处理的边际化估计方程方法）；基于工作假设推导控制I类错误且保证检验效能的样本量公式；通过广泛模拟评估公式的操作特性及对假设偏离的稳健性；提供实际应用指南。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提出的样本量公式在标称水平下能有效控制I类错误率并达到目标效能，且对部分工作假设的偏离具有一定的稳健性。在Drink Less MRT的真实数据分析中成功演示了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为具有分类处理和二元近端结局的MRT提供了完整的分析框架，包括效应定义、估计和样本量规划，有助于确保实际mHealth研究中的统计效能，推动数字健康干预的科学评估。

Abstract: Micro-randomized trials (MRTs) provide a framework for evaluating the marginal and moderated effects of mobile health (mHealth) interventions. In many applications, treatments take the form of categorical variables with multiple levels, such as different message contents or delivery strategies. Many scientifically meaningful longitudinal outcomes in mHealth studies are binary, such as whether a participant opens an app, engages with content, or completes a target behavior following a decision point at which treatment is randomized. This paper focuses on MRTs with categorical treatments and binary proximal outcomes. We define the causal excursion effect, propose an estimator called EMEE-catA, and derive a sample size formula for comparing categorical treatment levels that controls the type I error rate and guarantees power under working assumptions. We conduct extensive simulation studies to evaluate the operating characteristics of the proposed sample size formula, including robustness to violations of these assumptions. We further provide practical guidance for implementing the proposed approach to ensure adequate power in real-world MRTs. The methods are illustrated using data from the Drink Less MRT.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [337] [Statistical learning theory and Occam's razor: Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04049)
*Tom F. Sterkenburg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论出发，为奥卡姆剃刀原则——即在归纳推理中应偏好简洁性——提供了一种方法论上的正当性辩护，论证了在拟合度与简洁性之间进行权衡（即正则化）的必要性，这种辩护既非纯粹实用主义，也非基于"简单即真理"的本体论假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥卡姆剃刀原则在科学哲学和机器学习领域虽备受关注，但一直缺乏令人信服的正当性辩护。作者旨在填补这一空白，为"以简洁性换取拟合度"的正则化方法提供一个严格的理论基础。

Method: 基于早期"核心论证"（core argument），作者运用统计学习理论（statistical learning theory）展开手段-目的式论证（means-ends argument）：为了获得理论可靠性（theoretical reliability）和"所见即所得"（what-you-see-is-what-you-get）的保证，必须对简洁性施以某种优先于拟合度的偏好。

Result: 该论证表明，偏好简洁性是一种真正的方法论要求，它既不会退化为"为简洁而简洁"的纯粹实用主义原则，也不会依赖于"真理本身是简单的"这一本体论假设。

Conclusion: 正则化中的简洁性偏好可通过统计学习理论获得方法论上的正当辩护，这为奥卡姆剃刀原则提供了独立的、非本体论的理论支撑。

Abstract: The principle of Occam's razor, which instructs us to prefer simplicity in inductive inference, has attracted much scrutiny both in the philosophy of science and in machine learning. In either field, however, a justification for the principle has been elusive. In this paper, building on an earlier "core argument," I spell out a justification from statistical learning theory for the procedure of regularization: for trading off fit for simplicity. The means-ends argument is that in order to profit from theoretical reliability and "what-you-see-is-what-you-get" guarantees, one must implement a certain preference for simplicity over fit. This is a genuine methodological justification, which neither collapses to a purely pragmatic principle that we prefer simplicity for its own sake, nor to an ontological assumption that the truth is simple.

</details>


### [338] [Automatic Statistical Test for Rationally Expressible Algorithms by Selective Inference, with Applications to Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04667)
*Teruyuki Katsuoka,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Shuichi Nishino,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AutoSI 是一个自动化选择性推断框架，通过自动从算法操作中构建选择事件，将精确 SI 的适用范围从线性/二次不等式约束扩展到任意有理函数可表达的算法，并证明其 p 值在有限样本下精确有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精确选择性推断方法需要专家为每种新算法手工推导选择事件（即假设被选中的条件），且仅适用于由线性或二次不等式表征的选择事件，导致精确 SI 的应用范围极为有限。亟需一种自动化、通用化的框架来消除这一障碍。

Method: 提出 AutoSI 框架：(1) 自动将用户编写的 NumPy 风格算法代码中的每步操作转化为选择事件的构造过程，无需手工推导；(2) 将可处理的选择事件类别从线性/二次不等式扩展到数据的有理函数（多项式之比），从而覆盖更广泛的算法；(3) 在有限样本下证明 p 值的精确有效性。

Result: 在三个特征选择方法上验证 AutoSI，每种方法仅需几十行代码。其中，基于交叉验证 R² 选择调参的 Lasso 是现有精确 SI 框架无法处理的，而 AutoSI 成功实现了对其的推断。合成与真实数据实验表明，所得 p 值在名义水平上控制了第 I 类错误率，同时保持高统计功效。

Conclusion: AutoSI 通过自动构造选择事件和扩展可处理算法的类别，显著降低了精确选择性推断的使用门槛，使 SI 能够应用于更广泛的数据驱动假设选择场景，具有重要的方法论和实用价值。

Abstract: Selective inference (SI) provides statistically valid $p$-values for hypotheses selected by applying an algorithm to the data, correcting for the bias that arises when the same data are used both to select and to test a hypothesis. Developing an SI procedure for a new algorithm, however, has required an expert to derive, and then implement, the selection event, i.e., the conditions under which the hypothesis is selected. Repeating this specialized effort for every new algorithm is why exact SI has so far been available for only a narrow class. We propose AutoSI, a framework that removes this barrier in two ways. First, AutoSI constructs the selection event automatically from the algorithm's individual operations, so the user only writes the algorithm as ordinary NumPy-like code and derives nothing by hand. Second, AutoSI broadens the class of selection events SI can handle: existing exact methods are limited to selection events characterized by linear or quadratic inequalities in the data, whereas AutoSI covers any algorithm expressible through rational functions of the data (ratios of polynomials). We prove that the $p$-values computed by AutoSI are exactly valid in finite samples. We demonstrate AutoSI on three feature-selection methods, each written in a few dozen lines of code. One of these methods, the lasso with its tuning parameter selected by cross-validated $R^2$, cannot be handled within existing exact SI frameworks and is made possible by AutoSI. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the resulting $p$-values control the type I error rate (i.e., the false positive rate) at the nominal level while retaining high power.

</details>


### [339] [Intrinsic-Hybrid Latent Diffusion Models for Generative Modeling on Unknown Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04827)
*Yizhu Wang,Mu Niu,Xiaochen Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出内蕴混合潜在扩散模型(ILDM)，将概率维度约简与未知流形上的几何感知扩散相结合。通过将潜在空间解释为黎曼流形的坐标卡，并利用概率解码器量化几何与不确定性，前向过程在黎曼与欧几里得动力学之间混合切换。实验表明ILDM在COIL-100、MNIST和心脏MRI数据集上显著提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型需要大量训练数据且忽略数据的内在几何结构；潜在扩散模型虽降低维度，但强制施加欧几里得结构，无法捕捉底层流形几何，尤其在数据稀疏时问题突出。ILDM旨在同时解决高维数据生成中的维度灾难和几何结构缺失问题。

Method: 1) 将潜在空间建模为未知黎曼流形的坐标卡，通过概率解码器量化局部几何与不确定性；2) 设计混合扩散前向过程，依据局部不确定性在黎曼扩散与欧几里得扩散之间自适应切换，其中黎曼分量由解码器导出的概率度量张量控制；3) 提出适用于混合扩散设定的近似去噪得分匹配方法，推导混合Langevin动力学以定义逆向生成过程。

Result: 在COIL-100、MNIST和心脏MRI三个数据集上，ILDM相比标准扩散模型和潜在扩散模型均取得了更低的FID和LPIPS分数，生成质量显著提升。

Conclusion: ILDM通过将流形几何先验与不确定性量化融入潜在扩散框架，有效克服了数据稀疏条件下几何结构缺失的局限，为高维数据生成提供了一种几何感知的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce the Intrinsic Hybrid Latent Diffusion Model (ILDM), a generative framework that integrates probabilistic dimensionality reduction with geometry-aware diffusion on unknown manifolds. While diffusion models (DMs) have achieved state-of-the-art results in high-dimensional data synthesis, they rely on large training datasets and ignore intrinsic geometric structure. Latent diffusion models (LDMs) address the high dimensionality by learning a latent space, but they typically impose a Euclidean structure, failing to capture the underlying manifold geometry, especially problematic in data-sparse regimes. ILDM addresses these limitations by interpreting the latent space as a chart of an unknown Riemannian manifold, with geometry and uncertainty quantified through a probabilistic decoder. The forward process is a hybrid diffusion that switches between Riemannian and Euclidean dynamics based on local uncertainty, where the Riemannian component is governed by a probabilistic metric tensor derived from the decoder. To learn the generative dynamics, we introduce an approximate denoising score matching method tailored to the hybrid diffusion setting, enabling a backward process defined by hybrid Langevin dynamics. Experiments on COIL-100, MNIST, and cardiac MRI datasets demonstrate that ILDM significantly improves generation quality, achieving lower FID and LPIPS scores compared to standard diffusion and latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [340] [Stable Density Ridges: Consistency and Convergence of Subspace Constrained Mean Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05112)
*Wanli Qiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了SCMS算法并非收敛于文献中普遍认为的"静态密度脊"，而是收敛于作者新提出的"稳定密度脊"这一动力学系统结构；并据此构建了具有线性收敛性和更高计算效率的广义SCMS框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中长期存在一个广泛但未经严格验证的信念：SCMS算法的轨迹收敛于由密度梯度与Hessian矩阵特征系统定义的"静态密度脊"。作者发现该假设在一般情况下不成立，因为静态定义未考虑算法底层向量场连续流动中尾随特征空间的旋转效应，因此需要修正理论目标并重新建立算法的基础。

Method: 首先，通过引入动力学系统视角和投影密度梯度的雅可比矩阵，定义"稳定密度脊"作为SCMS的真实理论目标，并给出严格证明。其次，构建采用恒定步长的广义SCMS框架，证明其具有一致R-线性收敛性和到稳定脊的拓扑满射性。进一步推导用Hausdorff距离度量的稳定脊估计收敛速率。最后，揭示原始SCMS因通过均值漂移算子将步长与平滑带宽隐式耦合而导致多项式时间复杂度，并展示广义框架如何实现统计一致且更高效的解决方案。

Result: (1) 证明了SCMS的真实收敛目标是稳定密度脊而非静态密度脊；(2) 广义SCMS框架具有一致R-线性收敛性及拓扑满射性；(3) 给出了稳定脊估计的Hausdorff距离收敛速率；(4) 揭示了原始SCMS的多项式时间复杂度问题，广义框架在保持统计一致性的同时显著提升效率。

Conclusion: 该工作从根本上纠正了SCMS算法的理论基础，通过稳定密度脊的新概念弥合了静态定义与算法实际行为之间的鸿沟；所提出的广义SCMS框架不仅具有严格的理论保证，还解决了原始算法的计算效率瓶颈，对高维数据的非参数密度脊估计具有重要的理论与实践意义。

Abstract: The Subspace Constrained Mean Shift (SCMS) algorithm is a popular nonparametric method for extracting density ridges, which serve as a low-dimensional representation of high-dimensional data. It is a widely held belief in the literature that SCMS trajectories converge to the classical density ridge, which we call the "static ridge", defined via the density gradient and the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the density's Hessian. In this paper, we demonstrate that this assumption does not hold in general, as the static definition fails to account for the rotation of the trailing eigenspace along the continuous flow of the algorithm's underlying vector field. To resolve this, we propose a paradigm shift by introducing the "stable ridge", a novel geometric structure defined through the lens of dynamical systems and the Jacobian of the projected density gradient. We prove that this stable ridge is the true theoretical target of the SCMS algorithm. Building upon this foundation, we develop a generalized SCMS framework utilizing a constant step size, establishing its uniform R-linear convergence and topological surjectivity onto the stable ridge. We further derive the rates of convergence for estimating the stable ridge in terms of the Hausdorff distance. Finally, we expose that the original SCMS algorithm suffers from polynomial-time computational complexity, which is caused by implicitly coupling the step size to the smoothing bandwidth via the Mean Shift operator, and demonstrate how our generalized framework provides a statistically consistent and more efficient solution.

</details>
